<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）正快速演变为统一的理解与生成（UAG）范式，但将这些统一范式扩展到医学领域面临两大障碍：缺乏全面的训练和评估基准，以及缺乏广泛验证的统一医学模型。

Method: 1) 构建MedUAGCorpus：迄今最大的医学统一理解与生成数据集，包含超过600万实例、覆盖14种成像模态；2) 提出MedUAGBench：系统化基准，在标准化协议下将医学生成评估扩展到12个多样化任务；3) 开发MedUAG：端到端训练的统一医学模型，综合利用上述数据与基准资源进行训练。

Result: 大量实验表明MedUAG在广泛的理解和生成任务上取得了强劲的性能，建立了具有竞争力的基线结果。

Conclusion: MedUAG为下一代医学多模态系统奠定了基础，为医学领域统一理解与生成范式提供了全面的数据、基准和模型支撑。

Abstract: Recent Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are rapidly evolving into unified understanding and generation (UAG) frameworks. However, extending these unified paradigms to the medical domain is hindered by: the absence of comprehensive training and evaluation benchmarks, and the lack of broadly validated unified medical model. To address these gaps, we present a comprehensive foundation for medical UAG. First, we construct MedUAGCorpus, the largest unified medical understanding and generation dataset to date, comprising over 6 million instances across 14 imaging modalities. Second, we introduce MedUAGBench, a systematic benchmark that expands medical generation evaluation to 12 diverse tasks under standardized protocols. Finally, leveraging these resources, we develop MedUAG, an end-to-end trained unified medical model. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MedUAG achieves strong performance across a wide array of understanding and generation tasks, establishing a competitive baseline and paving the way for next-generation medical multimodal systems.

</details>


### [63] [Institutional Newspapers Pipeline: Deriving billions of high quality tokens from historical newspapers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18972)
*Matteo Cargnelutti,Catherine Brobston,Eben English,Jake Sadow,Kacie Bailey,Greg Leppert,Amanda Watson,Jessica Chapel,Jonathan Zittrain*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了与波士顿公共图书馆联合设计的Institutional Newspapers Pipeline模块化系统，用于从历史报纸扫描件中提取高质量、结构化数据。该流水线包含分割、OCR、文本分析、类型分类、阅读顺序检测、命名实体识别、主题分类、语言检测和嵌入生成等多个步骤，在1,473,635份公共领域报纸扫描件上运行，产出了83.1百万个crops和163亿tokens的开放数据集，并发布了配套的流水线、模型和数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史报纸是公共生活的丰富记录，但其密集、不规则的版面布局使得计算化访问既具挑战性又受限。现有系统往往难以在保持可解释性和可定制性的同时，从大规模历史报纸扫描件中提取高质量结构化数据。

Method: 设计了一个模块化、可解释且可定制的流水线，每步均可在工作站级硬件上运行。流水线流程包括：将扫描件分割为类型感知的分段（crops）、对每段进行OCR、随后对每个crop执行文本分析、类型分类、阅读顺序检测、命名实体识别、主题分类、语言检测及预计算嵌入生成。

Result: 在波士顿公共图书馆的部分馆藏上运行后，发布了开放数据集：共处理1,473,635份1795-1930年间公共领域报纸扫描件，提取83.1百万个crops，OCR输出约163亿个token。同时发布了训练的小型模型、评估结果和数据集规模测量数据。

Conclusion: 这项工作被认为是解锁数千万份报纸扫描件高质量数据的重要一步，为推动大规模历史报纸的数字化计算分析提供了可复现、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Historical newspapers are an abundant record of public life, but their dense, irregular and sometimes noisy layouts make computational access to these materials both challenging and limited. We present the Institutional Newspapers Pipeline, a modular system we jointly designed with Boston Public Library to extract high-quality, structured datasets from historical newspaper scans. It was architected so that each step remains interpretable and customizable, and so that the pipeline as a whole remains computationally frugal enough to run on workstation-level hardware. The pipeline runs each scan through a multi-step process: it segments scans into individual type-agnostic crops and performs OCR on each resulting segment before then performing text analysis, type classification, reading order detection, named entities recognition, subject classification, language detection, and pre-computed embeddings generation on every crop. We ran this pipeline against a portion of Boston Public Library's holdings and released the results as an open dataset. The optical character recognition (OCR) output represents 16.3 billion o200k_base tokens across 83.1 million individual crops, extracted from 1,473,635 public domain newspaper scans published between 1795 and 1930. This report describes our methods for each processing step, the small models we trained, as well as the evaluation results and dataset-scale measurements we collected in the process. It accompanies the release of the pipeline, models, and dataset. We position this work as a substantial step towards unlocking high-quality data from tens of millions of newspaper scans.

</details>


### [64] [DeepWeaver: Bridging the Evidence Synthesis Gap in Open-Ended Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18988)
*Xujia Wang,Yizhe Zhang,Bin Xu,Lei Hou,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DeepWeaver框架，通过维护Thought Block Chains（TBCs）——一种结构化表示，将检索到的嘈杂证据编织成语义连贯、引用充分且细节丰富的综合答案，解决了检索增强生成中"证据综合差距"（evidence synthesis gap）的问题，并引入高密度基准LoQA进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索-生成流水线常用于开放式问题的深度研究回答，但直接生成往往存在三大问题：证据利用不足（underuses evidence）、引用错位（misaligns citations）、多样化信息被压缩成浅层摘要（collapses into shallow summaries）。这暴露了检索与生成之间的"证据综合差距"——仅靠检索是不够的，LLM必须组织嘈杂的碎片化证据形成全面且有据可引的答案。

Method: DeepWeaver通过维护Thought Block Chains（TBCs）来结构化地编织证据。TBCs将声明（claims）、显著信息（salient information）、关键词（keywords）和支持证据（supporting evidence）分组。框架使用从属TBCs（subordinate TBCs）来检查残差证据（inspect residual evidence）、提交TBC修订（commit TBC revisions），并在最终生成前发现新声明（discover new claims）。

Result: 在多个LLM上，DeepWeaver在LoQA基准上提升了内容充分性（content sufficiency）、引用接地性（citation grounding）和细节保留（detail preservation）；在DeepResearch Bench上实现了更深入的洞察和更高的引用质量。同时引入了高密度的证据综合基准LoQA。

Conclusion: 证据编织（evidence weaving）是一种有效的机制，能够弥合开放式问答中检索与生成之间的鸿沟，验证了通过结构化中间表示（TBCs）进行证据综合的范式。

Abstract: Retrieve-then-generate pipelines are commonly used to produce deep-research answers for open-ended questions, but retrieval alone is insufficient: LLMs must organize noisy and fragmented evidence into comprehensive, well-cited answers. We refer to this process as evidence synthesis. However, direct generation often underuses evidence, misaligns citations, and collapses diverse information into shallow summaries, exposing an evidence synthesis gap between retrieval and generation. Thus, we propose DeepWeaver, a novel framework that weaves noisy retrieved evidence into comprehensive answers by maintaining Thought Block Chains (TBCs), a structured representation that groups claims, salient information, keywords, and supporting evidence. DeepWeaver uses subordinate TBCs to inspect residual evidence, commit TBC revisions, and discover new claims before final generation. We evaluate DeepWeaver on open-ended QA over both knowledge bases and the web, and introduce LoQA, a high-density benchmark for evidence synthesis. Across multiple LLMs, DeepWeaver improves content sufficiency, citation grounding, and detail preservation on LoQA, while achieving deeper insights and higher citation quality on DeepResearch Bench. These results show that evidence weaving is an effective mechanism for bridging retrieval and generation in open-ended QA. Our code is available at https://github.com/KlozeWang/DeepWeaver.

</details>


### [65] [Structure, Association, and Decision Value: Representation-Based Difficulty Estimation for Adaptive Inference in African-Language NLI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19003)
*Toheeb Ogunade*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探究内部表示统计量能否为多语言非洲NLP中的自适应推理提供示例级难度信号，发现在该设定下不能。作者报告了四个结果：AfriXNLI与XNLI测试数据高度重叠导致污染；参数数量不能可靠地排序非洲语言能力；角分散度比有效秩更受语言决定；有效秩与置信度分别预测不同类型的计算收益且二者仅相关0.655。最终结论是，没有任何被评估的信号能使自适应路由优于始终使用昂贵推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言非洲NLP中，自适应推理系统需要示例级别的难度信号来决定是否升级到更昂贵的模型。作者想知道内部表示统计量是否能提供这样的信号，从而在保持性能的同时降低计算成本。

Method: 在15种非洲语言上进行自然语言推断任务，使用冻结的现成checkpoint，考察多种表示统计量（角分散度、有效秩、廉价模型置信度）与两种计算收益目标（概率增益、预测是否改变）之间的关系，并进行语言控制后的相关分析。

Result: 四个核心发现：(1) AfriXNLI的英语配置与XNLI测试数据有1047/1050重叠，且一个广泛使用的checkpoint在该测试集上得分为1.000，表明存在测试数据暴露；(2) 较大checkpoint在7种语言上更好、8种语言上更差，无显著总体差异；(3) 角分散度比有效秩更受语言决定，池化相关性会膨胀前者掩盖后者；(4) 有效秩预测概率增益但不预测预测是否改变，廉价模型置信度反之，两目标仅相关0.655。在测试条件下，无信号使自适应路由优于始终使用昂贵推理，但oracle在60%计算量下高出11个准确率点。

Conclusion: 核心方法论发现是：一个表示统计量可能对某一种计算收益概念统计显著，但对另一种完全无关，因此作为决策变量可能失效。在当前的模型、信号和计算预算下，内部表示统计量不适合作多语言非洲NLP中自适应推理的示例级难度信号。

Abstract: We ask whether internal representation statistics can provide useful example-level difficulty signals for adaptive inference in multilingual African NLP, and find that they cannot in this setting. Studying natural language inference across 15 African languages with frozen off-the-shelf checkpoints, we report four results. First, AfriXNLI's English configuration shares 1,047 of its 1,050 examples verbatim with XNLI evaluation data, and one widely used NLI checkpoint scores 1.000 on that test split, consistent with XNLI test exposure. Because AfriXNLI is derived from XNLI, its English, French and Swahili configurations cannot serve as clean evaluations for XNLI-trained models. Second, parameter count does not reliably order capability across African languages: our larger checkpoint is better in seven languages and worse in eight, with no significant aggregate difference. Third, across three multilingual representation spaces, angular dispersion is consistently more language-determined than effective rank, so pooled correlations can inflate one and mask the other. Fourth, the association that survives language control depends on the target: effective rank predicts probability gain from escalation but not whether escalation changes the prediction, while cheap-model confidence shows the opposite pattern; the two targets correlate at only 0.655. Under the tested models, signals, and compute budgets, no evaluated signal makes adaptive routing preferable to always-expensive inference, although an oracle exceeds it by 11 accuracy points at 60% of the compute. Our central methodological finding is that a representation statistic can be statistically significant for one notion of computational benefit while being irrelevant to another, and therefore be a poor decision variable.

</details>


### [66] [Introducing the Privacy-HSD Trade-off: Hate Speech Detection, but not at the Cost of Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19006)
*Stephen Meisenbacher,Vlad Garbuz,Chirill Donos,Maxim Dnestreanschii,Gabriel Creanga,Andreea-Elena Bodea,Thomas Lampert,Jana Diesner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了仇恨言论检测（HSD）系统与用户隐私之间的权衡关系，发现HSD系统可能以牺牲作者身份隐私为代价获得性能，提出了隐私-HSD权衡的概念，并基准测试了多种文本私有化方法及新提出的领域特定AgnoSpeech技术，证明平衡隐私与HSD虽然困难但可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络仇恨言论对大量用户（尤其是青年和少数群体）构成现实威胁，建立可靠的自动仇恨言论检测系统至关重要；但作者认为这必须与个人隐私权相平衡。当前缺乏对HSD系统可能以编码作者身份信息为代价获得性能这一隐私威胁的认识，需要探索隐私与HSD之间的权衡。

Method: 首先演示HSD系统可能在编码作者身份方面牺牲隐私以换取性能；在此基础上提出隐私-HSD权衡的概念；随后基准测试一系列文本私有化方法，并提出了新的领域特定私有化技术AgnoSpeech，用于在保护隐私的同时维持HSD性能。

Result: 证明了HSD系统可能以牺牲作者身份隐私为代价获得性能表现；通过基准测试表明，在隐私保护与HSD性能之间取得平衡虽然困难但确实可行，其中新提出的AgnoSpeech技术提供了有效的解决方案。

Conclusion: 研究结果为隐私与HSD之间权衡的研究提供了有力案例，强调两者对保障在线参与的安全都具有实际影响，呼吁学界进一步深入研究这一权衡问题。

Abstract: Hate speech is a real and timely threat that affects a large portion of online users, especially youth and minority groups. While building reliable and robust automatic hate speech detection (HSD) systems is paramount, we argue that this must also be balanced with the individual right to privacy. Exploring the intersection of HSD and privacy, we demonstrate that HSD systems might unintentionally achieve performance at the cost of encoding authorship, posing a threat to privacy. Building on these findings, we establish the notion of a privacy-HSD trade-off, which demands a careful balance. We benchmark a series of text privatization methods, as well as our newly proposed domain-specific AgnoSpeech technique, showing that balancing privacy and HSD is difficult but feasible. The findings make a strong case for more research on the trade-offs between privacy and HSD, both of which have tangible implications for the safeguarding of online participation.

</details>


### [67] [Grading the Graders: Verification Autonomy Levels (L0-L5) for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19009)
*Yajie Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出验证自主等级（VAL）元标准，统一了LLM验证文献中"level"一词的五种不同含义，将验证方案按"验证规范的来源与判定保证"这一单一轴从L0到L5分级，并指出完备性盲区——替换与采样型验证器只能确认候选成立，无法证明无遗漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM常与验证器（步骤检查器、自一致性过滤器、基于工具的事实检查器、形式化证明助手）配对使用以检测模型错误，但验证文献中"level"一词混用了至少五种不同含义：验证粒度、概念抽象、风险等级、系统栈层次、以及真值来源的认知层面。这种术语混淆阻碍了不同验证方案之间的比较与评估，亟需一个统一的元标准来分类和厘清验证方案。

Method: 作者提出验证自主等级（VAL）元标准，沿单一轴（验证规范来源及判定保证）对验证方案分级：L0（LLM自声明，无确定性锚点）→ L2（客观真值，仅正确性）→ L3/L4（可判定系统，单属性或领域级完备性），并在无限制情形下L5不可达。文章通过四个领域（符号数学、行为监控、医学诊断、代码生成）及最强现有形式化验证基线进行实证分析，同时论证粒度、概念层级、风险和系统栈维度与VAL正交。

Result: 文章识别出文献未明确的二分法：完备性仅对形式化可规约的属性可达，而经验性开放世界验证（事实核查、诊断）封顶于锚定正确性（L2）。此外，验证器存在"完备性盲区"：基于替换和采样的验证器可以确认候选成立，但无法证明没有候选被遗漏。通过对17篇文献的调查，证明粒度、概念层级、风险和系统栈等级与VAL正交，解决了系统性的术语混淆问题。

Conclusion: VAL提供了一种清晰的元标准用于分类LLM验证方案，区分了验证的自主性等级与表面上正交的其他维度。该框架揭示了验证能力的根本边界：完备性在开放世界不可达，仅在形式化规约下有界可达。作者已发布代码和完整评估作为补充材料。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly paired with verifiers (step checkers, self-consistency filters, tool-based fact checkers, formal proof assistants) that claim to detect the model's errors. Yet the verification literature uses the word "level" to mean at least five different things: verification granularity, concept abstraction, risk tier, system-stack layer, and the epistemic source of the ground truth. We propose Verification Autonomy Levels (VAL), a meta-standard classifying verification schemes along a single axis: where does the verification spec come from, and what does the verdict guarantee? VAL ranges from L0 (LLM self-declaration, no deterministic anchor) through L2 (objective ground truth, correctness only) to L3/L4 (decidable systems with single-property or domain-level completeness), with L5 impossible in the unrestricted case. Central to VAL is the completeness blind spot: substitution- and sampling-based verifiers can confirm that proposed candidates hold, but cannot prove that no candidate was missed. We further identify a dichotomy the literature has not stated: completeness is reachable only for formally specifiable properties, while empirical open-world verification (fact-checking, diagnosis) caps at anchored correctness (L2). We document this across four domains (symbolic mathematics, behavior monitoring, medical diagnosis, and code generation) and in the strongest existing formal-verification baseline, whose authors note the verifier "focuses on the correctness of each step." We show the levels of granularity, concept hierarchy, risk, and system stack are orthogonal to VAL, resolving a systematic conflation across 17 surveyed papers. Code and full assessment are released as supplementary material.

</details>


### [68] [Institutional Books - Enriched Text: A customizable multilingual open-source pipeline for denoising, deduplicating, and annotating OCR text at scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19026)
*David Lowry-Duda,Matteo Cargnelutti,Catherine Brobston,Salwa Ismail,Greg Leppert,Amanda Watson,Jonathan Zittrain*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了"富文本"（Enriched Text）处理方法在哈佛图书馆Google Books数字化馆藏（IB-HL，约98.3万卷，242B token）中的应用。不同于传统流水线生成单一"完整"token流，该方法对文本进行归一化的同时，通过HTML风格注释保留元数据（按段落分离、逐段落语言检测、重复段落聚类、每段落bits-per-byte评分），使用户可根据自身需求定制输出而非接受全局编辑决策。发布内容包括IB-HL-ET富文本版本（217B token，98.3万卷，1.39B带注释子主题段落）及其生成流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IB-HL作为一个重要的大型人文数字馆藏，现有大规模预处理流水线多针对网页文本优化，倾向于激进过滤、去重、语言限制和丢弃元数据，这与细致的信息管理目标存在张力。同时研究者重复进行类似的处理和解析工作，缺乏标准化、灵活的基础设施。

Method: 提出"富文本"（Enriched Text）方法：不生成单一"完整"token流，而是对文本归一化并保留元数据注释。具体包括：分离书尾附属材料（endmatter）、检测每段语言、识别重复段落聚类、计算每段bits-per-byte分数。所有信息通过HTML风格注释叠加在文本上，用户可通过解析注释按需定制输出。该流水线适用于馆藏中约250种语言。

Result: 发布了IB-HL-ET：IB-HL的富文本版本，包含217B o200k_base tokens、983,003卷，组织为1.39B个带注释的子主题段落，同时公开了生成该版本的处理流水线。

Conclusion: 该工作通过将编辑决策从全局性预设转变为用户可定制的注释层，使大规模数字馆藏更容易被机器解析和供人类研究，在预处理效率与信息管理细致性之间取得了平衡。

Abstract: Released in 2025, Institutional Books: Harvard Library (IB-HL) is a collection of 983,004 volumes (242B o200k_base tokens), originally digitized through Harvard Library's participation in the Google Books Library project. As researchers and developers have begun to use IB-HL, a tension has emerged between standard large-scale preprocessing practices and the goals of careful information stewardship. Many existing pipelines optimize for web text: as a result, they tend to aggressively filter, deduplicate, restrict by language, and sometimes discard meaningful metadata. Meanwhile, researchers seeking to use IB-HL duplicate effort while performing similar processing and analysis.
  We describe an approach that we call Enriched Text. Instead of producing a single 'complete' stream of tokens, we normalize the text while preserving metadata through annotations. We separate endmatter, detect per-paragraph language, identify clusters of duplicate paragraphs, and compute per-paragraph bits-per-byte scores. We provide this information through HTML-like annotations layered on top of the text. By parsing these annotations, users can tailor the output to their own needs instead of accepting a global editorial decision on content. The pipeline applies to all $\approx$250 languages in the collection.
  This report describes this project's goals, implementation, and design rationale. The release includes IB-HL-ET (an enriched-text version of IB-HL containing 217B o200k_base tokens across 983,003 volumes, organized into 1.39B annotated subtopic paragraphs) and the pipeline that produced it. These serve to make the collection easier for machines to parse and for humans to study.

</details>


### [69] [Intercepting the Kangaroo: Experimental Astrolinguistics with Constructed Lexicons, Active Probing, and Large Language Models as Informants and Hypothesis Proposers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19124)
*Francesco Cordella,Mauro Cappelli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次将"天体语言学"（Astrolinguistics）从纯思辨变为可实验的研究。通过两个具有刻意不兼容词库的语言模型作为信息源，配合完全脚本化的编排器在不同范畴系统间翻译，作者提出并验证了一个结合跨情境消除、预注册预测探针、主动场景选择、严格恢复轮和隔离机制的协议，在400多次模拟与实景运行中实现零未检测误译，并能优雅降级、通过生成-测试循环恢复假设空间之外的词汇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自Freudenthal的Lincos（1960）以来，天体语言学——与范畴化方式不同于我们的心智进行交流——一直是纯思辨性的。作者试图将其变为实验科学，解决的核心问题是Quine翻译不确定性（袋鼠效应：词语被静默地错误附着到错误的指称对象上）的操作化验证。

Method: 采用两个词库刻意不兼容的语言模型作为拥有完整真值的信息源（一个编码形状、颜色和运动；另一个将颜色与运动融合、编码奇偶性并缺乏形状）。使用完全脚本化的编排器在两类范畴系统间翻译。核心协议包含五个组件：跨情境消除、预注册预测探针、主动场景选择、严格恢复轮和隔离机制。对于假设空间之外的词汇，采用生成-测试循环（LLM提出规则，脚本验证）。

Result: 在400多次模拟和实景运行中，完整协议在测试条件下实现了零未检测误译，覆盖率超出被动基线（d=0.62）。注入的袋鼠陷阱100%击败纯天真的指称和纯统计学习，但被完整协议全部拦截；在证据本体上不可得时声明Quinean等价类而非猜测。在信息源噪声下优雅降级：2%以下零袋鼠，10%时协议主要选择弃权而非犯错。外部词汇恢复覆盖率随LLM能力提升（0%→18%→72%→100%），未检测误译始终为零。

Conclusion: 在测试条件下，正确性是协议（protocol）的属性，而覆盖率是工具（instruments）的属性。即：协议的验证机制保证了翻译的正确性，翻译覆盖面取决于所使用模型的能力。

Abstract: Astrolinguistics -- communication with minds that categorize reality differently from ours -- has been purely speculative since Freudenthal's Lincos (1960). We make it experimental. Two language models with deliberately incompatible constructed lexicons (one encoding shape, color, and motion; the other fusing color with motion, encoding parity, and lacking shape) serve as informants with complete ground truth, while a fully scripted orchestrator translates between the two category systems. The central failure mode is the kangaroo effect: the silent attachment of a word to the wrong referent -- Quine's indeterminacy of translation, operationalized. Across 400+ simulated and live runs, a protocol combining cross-situational elimination, pre-registered predictive probes, active scene selection, a stricter recovery round, and quarantine produced no undetected mistranslations under the tested conditions and exceeded a passive baseline's coverage (d = 0.62). Injected kangaroo traps defeated naive ostension and pure statistical learning in 100% of runs, while the full protocol intercepted every decoy and, where discriminating evidence is ontologically unavailable, declared Quinean equivalence classes instead of guessing. Under informant noise it degrades gracefully: zero kangaroos persist up to 2% per-word noise; at 10% the protocol predominantly abstains rather than errs. Finally, words outside the scripted hypothesis space (a history-dependent relational term and an XOR contextual homonym) are recovered by a generate-and-test loop in which an LLM proposes rules and the script verifies them: coverage scales with proposer capability (0% -> 18% -> 72% -> 100%) while undetected mistranslations stayed at zero throughout. In the tested conditions, correctness is a property of the protocol; coverage is a property of the instruments.

</details>


### [70] [Comment-level Topic Drift Analysis in the Reddit Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19133)
*Steven Morse,Daniel Runfola,Trenton W. Ford*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于嵌入的动态主题建模方法，在12.7亿条Reddit评论（2006-2022）上分析评论级别的主题漂移。利用预训练语言模型生成上下文语义嵌入，通过无监督方法识别随时间演化的主题簇，并提出零模型对比检验来过滤虚假动态。研究发现政治和社会争议性话题在嵌入空间中表现出显著的方向性漂移，而音乐和体育等领域相对稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模在线语料中，语义和话语随时间演化，但现有方法难以在评论级别的大规模语料上有效检测和量化主题漂移。需要一种能够在嵌入空间本身中分析语义漂移和话语演化的方法。

Method: 1) 利用预训练语言模型为短文本生成上下文语义嵌入；2) 在嵌入上使用无监督聚类方法识别随时间动态演化的主题簇；3) 对现有动态主题建模方法进行修改，使其能在海量语料上规模化运行；4) 提出零模型对比检验以过滤虚假的动力学信号。

Result: 政治和社会争议性话题在嵌入空间中表现出显著方向性漂移，主题间距离随时间发生系统性变化，且超出零模型可解释的范围；音乐和体育等领域的主题则保持相对稳定。

Conclusion: 嵌入空间本身可以作为分析语义漂移和话语演化的有效载体，零模型对比检验能够显著提升动态主题分析的可靠性，这为大规模语料中的话语演化研究提供了新范式。

Abstract: We present a novel application of embedding-based dynamic topic modeling techniques to detect and quantify topic drift at the comment level in a massive corpus. By leveraging pretrained language models to generate contextualized semantic embeddings for short text, we analyzed 12.7 billion Reddit comments spanning 2006 to 2022. Using unsupervised methods on these embeddings, we identify dynamically evolving topic clusters over time. Our primary contribution is a methodology for analysis of semantic drift and discourse evolution in the embedding space itself. We also demonstrate modifications to existing methods that enable this analysis at scale, and we propose and demonstrate a null model comparison test to filter spurious dynamics. Key findings suggest that politically and socially contentious topics exhibit significant directional drift in embedding space, with inter-topic distances changing systematically over time beyond what the null model can explain, whereas domains such as music and sports remain comparatively stable.

</details>


### [71] [ChildSafeAds Shared Task 2026: Commercial Content in Child-Facing YouTube Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19165)
*Thales Bertaglia,Catalina Goanta,Gerasimos Spanakis,Gunes Acar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文介绍ChildSafeAds共享任务，旨在识别YouTube视频中对儿童和青少年可能具有商业推广内容。数据集包含939个频道的3,360个视频，每个视频以SponsorBlock用户标记的赞助片段开头，并配以转录文本、视频和频道信息及链接销售页面。系统需完成三个子任务：识别推广类型(ST1)、产品类别(ST2)和法律风险标记(ST3)。数据按四种累积访问级别划分证据，以便比较不同数据收集成本下的结果。生成标签过程中使用GPT-5.4和GPT-5.6-luna辅助标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿童和青少年是易受商业内容影响的群体，但YouTube等平台上针对这一群体的商业推广内容缺乏系统性识别和监管。现有的平台广告披露机制（如"包含付费推广"标签）使用率很低（仅54.5%正确使用），因此需要构建一个标准化的数据集和共享任务，来促进自动识别儿童可及视频中的商业内容和法律风险。

Method: 从SponsorBlock（开源众包浏览器扩展）收集用户标记的赞助片段，每个实例包含对应的转录文本、视频和频道信息，以及视频描述中链接的销售或服务页面。数据划分为四个累积访问级别（从转录文本到链接页面），用于评估不同信息获取成本下的系统性能。分类任务包括三个子任务：推广类型识别(ST1)、产品类别分配(ST2)和法律风险标识(ST3)。标签通过专家组织团队审查样本并迭代优化分类体系、提示和模型选择，使用GPT-5.4生成标签，GPT-5.6-luna独立标注开发集。

Result: 构建了包含3,360个视频、939个频道的多模态数据集，并发现45.5%的视频未正确使用平台内的广告披露方法（"包含付费推广"标签）。该共享任务包含三个子任务和四种累积数据访问级别，为研究社区提供了标准化的基准。

Conclusion: 该论文是ChildSafeAds共享任务的描述报告，介绍了任务目标、数据构建方法和评估方案。目前只报告了任务设计和数据情况，参与系统和共享任务结果将在更新版本中呈现。

Abstract: ChildSafeAds is a shared task on commercial content in YouTube videos likely to reach children and teenagers. It contains 3,360 videos from 939 channels. Each instance begins with a segment submitted to SponsorBlock, an open-source crowdsourced browser extension whose users mark sponsor segments so that others can skip them. We pair the segment with its available transcript, video and channel information, and a sales or service page linked from the video description. Systems determine what kind of offer is being promoted (ST1), assign product categories (ST2), and identify legal risk flags (ST3). The evidence is divided into four cumulative access levels, from the transcript to the linked page, so results can be compared against the cost of collecting the data. 45.5\% of videos in our data failed to properly use the in-platform ad disclosure method (the ``Includes paid promotion'' label). GPT-5.4 produced the labels after the expert organiser team reviewed samples and iterated on the taxonomy, prompts and model choices. GPT-5.6-luna independently labelled the development set. This report describes the task, data and evaluation. An updated version will add participating systems and shared-task results.

</details>


### [72] [SPADE: Self-Play in Adaptive Synthetic Executable Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19197)
*Bo Liu,Simon Yu,Yiding Jiang,Ao Qu,Andrew Zhao,Zichen Liu,Junsu Kim,Zijian Zhou,Seungone Kim,Tongzheng Ren,Mickel Liu,Hanfei Yu,Zhaorun Chen,Weiyan Shi,Paul Pu Liang,Luke Zettlemoyer,Yejin Choi,Natasha Jaques*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SPADE是一个自博弈强化学习框架，让单个LLM同时扮演环境设计者和推理智能体两个角色。环境设计者生成可执行的Gym风格长时程训练环境，推理智能体在其中学习；通过优化推理智能体的遗憾信号（有/无特权提示的奖励差距），环境设计者学会在智能体能力边界处生成可行环境。实验表明SPADE在数学、科学、代码、推理和工具使用等多类基准上超越最强固定环境基线，将环境设计本身变为可学习的组件，迈向开放式自我改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言智能体的训练环境池（手工设计、静态合成或冻结验证器构建的）在智能体规模扩展时保持目标分布固定，无法持续提供多样化的自适应目标，限制了连续自我改进的能力。

Method: SPADE采用自博弈强化学习框架：单个LLM扮演两个角色——(1) Environment Designer，生成完整的长时程训练环境（以OpenAI Gym风格的reset()/step()接口写成可执行代码，包含状态转换、奖励函数和验证代码）；(2) Reasoning Agent，在这些环境中学习行动。智能体的遗憾通过有/无特权提示（privileged hints）的奖励差距来估计，环境设计者通过优化该遗憾信号来针对性生成位于智能体能力边界且可行的环境。关键组件包括：将环境设计者接地在大规模预训练语料库采样的文档上，以及为环境设计者提供累积的环境记忆。

Result: 扩展到30B参数模型时，SPADE在八个留出基准（数学、科学、代码、推理）上平均比最强固定环境基线提升+5.3；工具使用场景中BFCL-v4多轮提升+5.7，ACEBench-Agent提升+13.9；游戏场景中优势随模型规模增长而扩大。消融实验发现语料库接地和环境记忆对成功至关重要。

Conclusion: 通过将环境设计本身变为可学习的组件，SPADE使训练环境池能够随智能体能力自适应演化，为开放式自我改进提供了具体可行的路径。这个框架统一了推理问题和多步智能体工具使用，因为两者都可以表示为有状态、多轮的环境接口。

Abstract: Continuous self-improvement requires an ever-expanding pool of self-generated, diverse, adaptive goals. For language agents, existing training environment pools (hand-curated, statically synthesized, or frozen-verifier) keep the goal distribution fixed as the learner scales. We introduce SPADE (Self-Play in Adaptive Synthetic Executable Environments), a self-play RL framework in which a single LLM plays two roles: an Environment Designer that writes complete, long-horizon training environments as executable code with an OpenAI Gym-style reset()/step() interface, and a Reasoning Agent that learns to act in them. Each is a stateful, multi-turn environment (state transitions, reward functions, and verification code), so one interface spans reasoning problems and multi-step agentic tool use. The Reasoning Agent's regret is estimated using the gap between its reward with and without privileged hints; in optimizing this regret signal the Environment Designer learns to target environments at the edge of the agent's capabilities while keeping them feasible. Through extensive experimentation, we find several components critical to success: grounding the Environment Designer on documents sampled from a large pretraining corpus, and giving it an accumulated environment memory. Scaling to 30B-parameter models, SPADE improves over the strongest fixed-environment baseline by +5.3 on average across eight held-out math, science, code, and reasoning benchmarks, and lifts the tool-use setting by +5.7 on BFCL-v4 multi-turn and +13.9 on ACEBench-Agent; on the games setting, the margin over the strongest baseline grows with model scale. By making environment design itself a learnable component, SPADE takes a concrete step toward open-ended self-improvement.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [73] [Optimal Loss Allocation in a Mean-Field Model of Systemic Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18113)
*Yucheng Guo,Qinxin Yan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究一个系统性风险控制问题：中央银行规划者将银行违约产生的损失分配至幸存机构。最优分配规则是"征税最富者"策略——将损失集中在当前最健康的银行上，使高于内生阈值的银行降至阈值水平。论文证明离散时间均值场控制问题的时间收敛性、刻画其极限为奇异均值场控制问题（含反射自由边界），并建立有限粒子系统的收敛基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统纾困(bailout)模型允许注入外部资本或减少总损失，但在许多现实情景下（如严格预算约束的监管机构）这类干预不可行。本文的动机是研究不引入外部资源、仅通过优化内生损失分配来控制系统性风险的问题，为监管实践提供一个在无外部救助能力下仍可操作的分配准则。

Method: 将银行建模为吸收布朗运动驱动的距离违约(distance-to-default)过程，叠加由违约损失再分配引发的向下跳跃。采用均值场控制(MFC)框架：在离散时间中证明最优分配规则为截止型/征税最富者策略（将高于内生效用阈值的银行削减至阈值）；通过让分配时间步趋于零建立极限，将其刻画为奇异均值场控制问题；用反射自由边界公式描述受控律，值函数满足Wasserstein空间上的Hamilton-Jacobi方程；最后构建有限粒子控制问题并证明其收敛到连续时间均值场模型。

Result: 主要结果：①最优分配规则具有简单经济解释——损失应集中于当前最健康的机构；②离散时间规则为截止型/征税最富者政策；③证明时间离散化均值场控制问题收敛，极限为奇异均值场控制问题，其受控律由反射自由边界描述；④值函数满足Wasserstein空间上的Hamilton-Jacobi方程；⑤有限粒子系统在该截止规则下收敛至连续时间均值场模型，为最优均值场损失分配规则提供有限系统基础。

Conclusion: 本文给出了一个无外部资本注入情形下的系统性风险损失最优分配理论。核心洞见是"让最强者承担最多"这一反直觉但在该约束下最优的策略，并建立了从离散时间到连续时间、从有限粒子到均值场的完整数学框架，为系统性风险监管提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study a systemic-risk control problem in which a central planner allocates losses generated by bank defaults across the surviving institutions. Banks are modeled through their distances to default, evolving as absorbed Brownian motions with downward jumps induced by redistributed default losses. Unlike bailout models, the planner cannot inject external capital or reduce the aggregate loss, and the only admissible intervention is to decide how each endogenous loss is assigned among solvent banks. The objective is to maximize terminal system health, including survival mass as a leading special case and, more generally, increasing concave welfare functionals of the terminal distribution.
  Our main result identifies an optimal allocation rule with a simple economic interpretation: losses should be concentrated on the currently healthiest institutions. In discrete time, this rule takes the form of a cutoff or taxing-the-richest policy, which reduces banks above an endogenous threshold down to that threshold while leaving weaker banks untouched. We prove convergence of the time-discretized mean-field control problem as the allocation time step tends to zero and characterize the limiting problem as a singular mean-field control problem. The optimally controlled law is described by a reflected free-boundary formulation, in which the cutoff becomes the moving upper edge of the support, and the associated value function satisfies a Hamilton-Jacobi equation on Wasserstein space. Finally, we formulate the corresponding finite-particle control problem and show, under suitable assumptions, that the cutoff-controlled particle system converges to the continuous-time mean-field model. This provides a finite-system foundation for the optimal mean-field loss-allocation rule.

</details>


### [74] [Formal Safety Verification for Nonlinear Systems with Generative Barrier Certificate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18118)
*Mengxin Ren,Hanrui Zhao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的生成式框架，用于合成非线性系统的屏障证书（BC）。该方法基于经典SOS方法训练领域专用LLM生成高质量BC候选，将难以求解的BMI问题转化为凸LMI可行性检验，实现了比传统数值方法快几个数量级的速度提升，并超越了最先进的神经BC模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出一个生成式框架：基于经典SOS方法训练一个领域专用的大语言模型（LLM），该LLM能够通过推理生成高质量的非线性系统BC候选。利用LLM生成的BC将不可解的BMI问题转化为凸的LMI可行性检验，从而大幅提升效率同时保证正确性。

This paper introduces a generative framework that leverages large language models (LLMs) to synthesize BCs through reasoning. Based on the classical Sum-of-Squares (SOS) approach, we train a domain-specific LLM capable of generating high-quality BC candidates for nonlinear systems. Then, the LLM-generated BCs transform the intractable Bilinear Matrix Inequality (BMI) solving problems into convex Linear Matrix Inequality (LMI) feasibility test, significantly improving efficiency while preserving correctness. Experimental results show that our generative method achieves several orders of magnitude speedup over traditional numerical BC approaches and, perhaps surprisingly, surpasses the state-of-the-art dedicated neural BC model. These findings mark a substantive step toward integrating generative AI with formal safety verification for dynamical systems.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在报童问题、合成投资组合、分布偏移和真实金融数据上的实验表明，学习并校准的歧义集能提高可靠性，但并不会自动产生更小的半径或更好的决策。校准应被视为实现可靠决策的实用机制，而非优化性能改善的普遍保证。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架将校准定位为可靠决策的实用工具，强调歧义集的关键要求是包含未知数据生成分布（以期望概率），而非以经验分布为中心。该框架在理论上提供了有限样本保证、渐近一致性、稳定性和误差分解，但实证表明学习式歧义集不必然优于传统方法。

Abstract: Wasserstein distributionally robust optimization (DRO) is commonly built around the empirical distribution, with the ambiguity radius selected from a concentration bound. Although this construction provides useful statistical guarantees, it can be conservative and does not fully exploit predictive information about the underlying distribution or the difficulty of a particular decision problem. We develop a more flexible framework in which a predictive model determines the nominal distribution and a separate model estimates a data-dependent radius. The key requirement is not that the ambiguity set be centered at the empirical distribution, but that it contain the unknown data-generating distribution with the desired probability. We establish finite-sample guarantees and asymptotic consistency for arbitrary learned centers, derive tractable reformulations for non-uniform discrete predictive distributions, separate predictive-model and scenario-discretization errors, and prove stability under simultaneous perturbations of the center and radius. We further characterize the oracle conditional-quantile radius as the smallest conditionally valid rule and introduce a split-conformal procedure for finite-sample marginal calibration. Experiments on newsvendor problems, synthetic portfolios, distribution shifts, and real financial data show that learned and calibrated ambiguity sets can improve reliability, but do not automatically yield smaller radii or better decisions. Overall, the proposed framework treats calibration as a practical mechanism for reliable decision making rather than a universal guarantee of improved optimization performance.

</details>

*Haoyu Yao,Pengcheng Xie*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出TOBYQA，一个时间增强的导数自由优化框架，通过在二次插值系统中联合建模空间几何与时间漂移，并引入岭正则项与漂移补偿比率测试，显著提升非静态环境下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: TOBYQA包含三个关键设计：(1) 在经典KKT插值系统的(1,1)块上引入脊项以应对观测噪声，在约束块的满列秩条件下确保适定性，放宽了经典方法的几何态势要求；(2) 在插值模型中嵌入显式的时间线性漂移项，设计漂移补偿比率测试，从观测减少量中减去估计的时间成分；(3) 采用对角仿射缩放使搜索几何适应局部曲率变化。

Result: 在Moré-Wild 88函数套件上，针对不同时间漂移水平和维度（n=6,10,20）的基准评估表明，TOBYQA在漂移环境下展现出增强的鲁棒性，同时在静态环境中保持有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: TOBYQA通过时间增强的二次插值建模框架，成功弥合了静态与动态优化之间的鸿沟，为存在时间漂移和噪声的实际DFO应用提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Derivative-free optimization (DFO) becomes particularly difficult when function evaluations are affected by temporal drift and observation noise. Conventional trust-region methods generally assume a static landscape, which may lead to stale-data bias and misleading trust-region updates. We propose TOBYQA (Time-augmented Optimization BY Quadratic Approximation), a regularized DFO framework that jointly models spatial geometry and temporal variations within a unified quadratic interpolation system. Specifically, TOBYQA augments the classical KKT interpolation system with a ridge term on the $(1,1)$-block, which accommodates observation noise and ensures well-posedness under a mild full-column-rank condition on the constraint block, thereby relaxing the stringent geometric poisedness requirements of classical methods. To handle non-stationarity, we embed an explicit linear-in-time drift term into the interpolation model, enabling a drift-compensated ratio test that subtracts the estimated temporal component from the observed reduction. Additionally, a diagonal affine-scaling adapts the search geometry to local variation in curvature. Benchmark evaluations on the Moré--Wild 88-function suite across various temporal drifts and dimensions ($n \in \{6,10,20\}$) demonstrate that TOBYQA exhibits enhanced robustness under drift, while maintaining competitive performance in static environments.

</details>

Result: 优化充电时间显著提升了能源效率，减少了对昂贵的系统支持和辅助服务的需求，验证了充电灵活性在降低电网失衡和促进可再生能源整合方面的实际效益。

Abstract: This study evaluates the potential of electric vehicle (EV) charging flexibility to enhance both energy efficiency and power grid stability. By aligning EV charging with periods of lower grid demand and higher renewable generation, we demonstrate a significant improvement in energy efficiency, reduc ing the need for costly system support and ancillary services. Our findings suggest that cooperation between power distributors and transmission system operators can unlock new opportunities for maintaining grid stability while promoting sustainable energy use in an increasingly uncertain energy landscape.

</details>

Method: 论文提出了两种方法：(1) 一种新的多起点局部搜索（multistart local search）方法用于解决在安全性约束下最大化效力的单目标优化问题；(2) 一种多目标局部搜索方法，同时优化效力和安全性两个目标，以揭示两者之间的权衡关系。

Abstract: CAR T-cell circuit design enables targeting tumour cells while keeping healthy cells unaffected to a cancer treatment. We provide a new multistart local search approach for this problem and show that it outperforms the current SOTA approach on a single-cell cancer atlas. Furthermore, we introduce a multi-objective local search approach which provides insights into the trade-offs of efficacy and safety of the treatment. Our experiments also demonstrate that the multi-objective approach yields improved solutions even for the constrained single-objective optimisation problem.

### [79] [Nearest Graph Laplacians with Prescribed Connected Components: A Convex Framework for Network Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18128)
*Udit Raj,Sudeepto Bhattacharya,Prince Kanhya*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究如何构造与给定拉普拉斯矩阵最近的、且具有指定连通分量结构的图拉普拉斯矩阵。通过施加块指示向量约束 MU=0 和块内连通性约束，将问题建模为严格凸的Frobenius范数目标半定优化问题，证明了最优解的存在性、唯一性以及精确的连通分量结构，并应用于Sampson修道院网络等数值示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际图网络分析中，经常需要将现有网络转换或重构为具有特定群组分离结构的网络，例如社团检测或群体分割。然而，如何在保持"最小干预"（即与原始拉普拉斯矩阵尽可能接近）的前提下，精确地强制实现预设的分组结构，尚缺乏严格的数学框架和求解方法。

Result: 证明了优化问题最小解的存在性与唯一性，并证明优化后的拉普拉斯矩阵恰好具有预设的连通分量结构，其零空间等于由块指示向量张成的子空间。数值实验（包括Sampson修道院正情感网络）展示了最近的分派一致加权重构结果，以及实现预设分派结构所需的最小拉普拉斯扰动。

Conclusion: 该框架为量化从图网络到指定群组分隔结构所需的最小结构干预提供了一种原理性工具，具有广泛的潜在应用场景。

Abstract: We study the problem of constructing the nearest graph Laplacian matrix to a given Laplacian while enforcing a prescribed connected-component structure. Let the vertex set be partitioned into nonempty disjoint blocks $C_1,\ldots,C_k$, and let $U=[u_1,\ldots,u_k]$ be the matrix of the corresponding block-indicator vectors. The constraint $MU=0$ ensures that these prescribed indicators lie in the nullspace of the optimized Laplacian $M^\star$, and hence the associated graph has at least $k$ connected components. To guarantee exactly the prescribed components, we impose additional block-connectivity constraints on the principal blocks $M_j=M[C_j,C_j]$. These constraints ensure that each prescribed block induces a connected weighted subgraph. The resulting problem is a convex semidefinite optimization problem with a strictly convex Frobenius-norm objective. We prove existence and uniqueness of the minimizer and show that the optimized Laplacian has exactly the prescribed connected components, with nullspace $\operatorname{span}\{u_1,\ldots,u_k\}$. The framework proposed in this work provides a principled tool for quantifying the minimum structural intervention required to transform a graph-based network into one having a prescribed group-separated structure. Numerical examples, including the Sampson monastery positive-affection network, illustrate the nearest faction-consistent weighted reconstruction and the minimum Laplacian perturbation required to realize the prescribed faction structure.

</details>


### [80] [Simulation-Optimization of Systems of Optimizers: Exploiting the Inner Optimization's Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18129)
*Zhou He*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究"优化器系统之仿真优化"(SOSO)，即每个智能体在每决策时刻都求解结构化优化问题（LP、混合整数规划或动态规划）的基于智能体的仿真。作者形式化了SOSO，并开发了利用内嵌优化几何结构的框架：证明内层LP的最优基和其对偶变量可通过动力学传播，对外层目标产生无偏、单次复制的无穷小扰动分析(IPA)梯度；推导出由基分歧计数控制的公共随机数协方差界；证明IPA方差随反馈深度指数增长（形式化了牛鞭效应）；提出带误差预算的替代作为决策方案统一LP-视界与强化学习替代。这些方法整合为PRIME随机逼近求解器。在涵盖SOSO分类法的六个测试平台上，PRIME在同等预算下取得最优或并列最优的优化间隙，近乎零振荡和窄种子方差；IPA比独立有限差分实现约2000倍的单复制方差缩减，公共随机数在1,000 SKU、六配送中心供应链中将配对差方差削减超10,000倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准仿真优化将仿真视为黑箱，丢弃了内层优化（LP/MIP/DP）的几何结构，而这些内层优化在供应链、电力市场和物流等系统中普遍存在且包含有用的数学信息，利用这些几何结构可以获得计算优势。

Method: 形式化SOSO框架；利用内层LP的最优基和对偶变量的传播来构造精确、无偏、单复制IPA梯度（仅以零测基变换为障碍）；推导公共随机数协方差界；分析IPA方差随反馈深度的指数增长并形式化牛鞭效应；引入surrogate-as-decision与误差预算来统一LP-视界和强化学习替代；组合为PRIME随机逼近求解器（IPA梯度+自适应步长+多起点空间多样化）。

Result: 在六个SOSO测试平台上，PRIME在同等预算下达到最佳或并列最佳优化间隙、近零振荡和窄种子间方差；IPA实现约2000倍的单复制方差缩减（相对独立有限差分）；公共随机数在1,000-SKU六配送中心供应链中将配对差方差削减超10,000倍；实验结果证实了利用内嵌优化几何结构作为仿真优化中具有实际意义的研究方向。

Conclusion: 利用内嵌优化几何结构可以显著提升仿真优化的效率与稳定性，该方向具有实际工程价值，为SOSO系统提供了理论保证且可扩展的求解范式。

Abstract: We study simulation-optimization of systems of optimizers (SOSO): agent-based simulations in which every agent solves a structured optimization - a linear program (LP), mixed-integer program, or dynamic program - at every decision epoch. Such systems arise in supply chains, electricity markets, and logistics, yet standard simulation optimization treats the simulation as a black box, discarding the inner optimization's geometry. We formalize SOSO and develop a framework that converts this geometry into computational advantage. We prove that the inner LP's optimal basis and dual variables propagate through the dynamics to yield an exact, unbiased, single-replication infinitesimal perturbation analysis (IPA) gradient of the outer objective, with measure-zero basis changes as the only obstructions. We derive a common-random-numbers covariance bound governed by a computable basis-disagreement count from the forward simulation. We further prove that IPA variance grows exponentially with feedback depth, formalizing the bullwhip effect, and introduce surrogate-as-decision with an error budget unifying LP-horizon and reinforcement-learning surrogates under one bound. We compose these into PRIME, a stochastic-approximation solver integrating the IPA gradient, adaptive step sizing, and multi-start spatial diversification. On six testbeds spanning the SOSO taxonomy, PRIME achieves the best or tied-best optimality gap at equal budget with near-zero oscillation and narrow seed-to-seed variance; IPA yields a ~2000x per-replication variance reduction over independent finite differences, and common random numbers cut paired-difference variance by over 10,000x in a 1,000-SKU, six-distribution-center supply chain. These results establish exploiting embedded-optimization geometry as a practically significant direction for simulation optimization.

</details>

*Xi Chen,Shixin Wang,Bingkun Zhou,Yuan Zhou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究可复用服务器容量与非平稳收益下的在线二分匹配问题。在局部有界收益假设下，提出了两种BALANCE类算法：TS-BAL通过最大化可行复用调度上的累积阻塞损失，达到2ln(δD)+O(lnln(δ∨D))的竞争比；GR-BAL使用该损失的贪心松弛，达到ln(δD)+O(lnln(δ∨D))的竞争比，与ln(δD)的下界在首项上匹配。实验表明算法在全局收益漂移和有限容量下表现稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线二分匹配的竞争比保证通常以全局奖励范围来校准，当收益在长时间尺度上漂移时，该范围可能变得任意大，导致已有的竞争比保证失去实际意义。因此需要引入了局部有界的收益条件，在更合理的假设下设计算法。

Method: 提出两种BALANCE类型算法：(1) TS-BAL（时间感知机会成本损失），最大化可行复用调度上的累积阻塞损失，达到2ln(δD)+O(lnln(δ∨D))竞争比；(2) GR-BAL，使用该损失的贪心松弛，达到ln(δD)+O(lnln(δ∨D))竞争比，在首项上与ln(δD)下界匹配。

Result: TS-BAL达到2ln(δD)+O(lnln(δ∨D))竞争比；GR-BAL达到ln(δD)+O(lnln(δ∨D))竞争比，匹配首项下界ln(δD)。数值实验证明在显著的全局收益漂移下仍表现稳健，且有限容量下性能良好。

Conclusion: 在局部有界收益条件下，BALANCE类算法能为可复用容量、非平稳收益的在线二分匹配提供接近最优的竞争比保证，且GR-BAL在首项上已达到下界，表明该条件下算法已接近理论最优。

Abstract: We study online bipartite matching with reusable server capacity and non-stationary rewards. Jobs arrive sequentially, reveal compatible servers, reward rates, and processing durations, and must be accepted or rejected irrevocably. An accepted job occupies one unit of server capacity only during its processing interval, so an assignment may displace an unknown sequence of future jobs. Existing guarantees are typically calibrated by a global reward range, which can become arbitrarily large when rewards drift over a long horizon. We instead impose a locally bounded reward condition: reward rates of jobs that can compete for the same server within a relevant time window differ by at most a factor $δ$. Under this condition, we develop two BALANCE-type algorithms with time-aware opportunity-cost losses. TS-BAL maximizes cumulative blocking losses over feasible reuse schedules and achieves a competitive ratio of $2\ln(δD)+\mathcal O(\ln\ln(δ\vee D))$. GR-BAL uses a greedy relaxation of this loss and achieves $\ln(δD)+\mathcal O(\ln\ln(δ\vee D))$, matching a lower bound of $\ln(δD)$ in the leading term. Numerical experiments demonstrate robust performance under substantial global reward drift and favorable finite-capacity performance.

</details>


### [82] [A Mode-wise Adaptive Observer for Autonomous Switched Nonlinear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18251)
*Juan Sereno,E. A. Hernandez-Vargas*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对一类具有切换未知参数的自适应切换非线性系统，提出了一种模态自适应观测器。通过为每个模态分配专用自适应观测器，消除了切换对参数误差动态的零输入扰动；引入有限窗口持续激励条件和最小驻留时间条件，保证了参数估计误差的收缩性和状态估计误差的有界性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数字孪生范式中，在线参数更新至关重要。数字孪生必须持续适应其所表征系统的动态演化，而自适应观测器能够从在线数据中联合估计状态和参数，因此日益重要。然而，对于具有切换未知参数的自适应切换非线性系统，存在两大挑战：一是切换会向参数误差动态注入扰动，二是需要在不依赖输入信号的情况下保证有限时间窗口内的持续激励条件。

Method: 本文提出为每个模态分配一个专用的自适应观测器，该观测器仅在其对应的时间区间内激活，从而直接消除切换引起的零输入扰动。同时，引入有限窗口持续激励条件和最小驻留时间条件，在这些条件下，参数估计误差具有收缩性，状态估计误差在每个模态的激活区间内保持有界。

Result: 通过一个学术示例和一个进化治疗的实用示例，验证了所提出方法的性能，展示了模态自适应观测器在切换非线性系统中联合估计状态和参数的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为数字孪生中的在线参数更新提供了一种有效的解决方案，通过模态专用自适应观测器设计，成功解决了切换引入的扰动问题和无输入信号下的持续激励问题，为自治切换非线性系统的参数与状态联合估计提供了理论保证。

Abstract: In the digital twin paradigm, online parameter updating is essential. Unlike a static model, a digital twin must continuously adapt to the evolving dynamics of the system it represents. Adaptive observers, which jointly estimate states and parameters from online data, are therefore an increasingly important tool. In this work, we formulate a mode-wise adaptive observer for a class of autonomous switched nonlinear systems with switched unknown parameters. The main challenges are twofold: removing the disturbance that switching injects into the parameter-error dynamics and guaranteeing persistence of excitation over a finite-time window without relying on an input signal. To address them, we assign a dedicated adaptive observer to each mode, active only on its corresponding interval, which directly removes the zero-input disturbance caused by switching. We then introduce a finite-window persistence-of-excitation condition together with a minimum dwell-time condition, under which the parameter estimation error is contractive and the state estimation error is bounded within the active interval of each mode. The performance of the proposed approach is illustrated with an academic example and with a practical example of evolutionary therapies.

</details>


### [83] [Self-supervised In-context Operator Learning for Stochastic Mean-Field Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18282)
*Suyi Gao,Mo Zhou,Rongjie Lai*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出NFIST，首个无网格、自监督的随机均值场控制(MFC)神经算子。通过将概率流ODE与基于归一化流的可逆变换器结合，将随机动力学转化为确定性连续方程，实现闭式分数评估(O(d)成本)。通过上下文学习中的任务提示条件化传输映射，使单一预训练算子能够在前向传播中一次性解决未见任务，无需预计算数值解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机MFC的数值和深度学习方法每次只能求解一个具体问题实例，任务变化时需要重新优化，缺乏对大规模任务族的高效泛化能力。本文旨在将随机MFC建模为算子学习问题，实现一次性预训练即可零样本泛化到全新任务。

Method: 将随机MFC重新表述为算子学习问题。核心创新包括：(1) 结合概率流ODE与可逆归一化流变换器，克服受控Fokker-Planck方程中扩散项阻碍确定性传输映射的挑战；(2) 通过归一化流的精确逆和解析对数行列式实现闭式分数评估，每个粒子O(d)成本；(3) 利用基于变换器的上下文学习，以紧凑分布参数或原始粒子云作为任务提示条件化传输映射；(4) 端到端训练，直接最小化随机控制目标，无需预计算数值解。

Result: 在随机最优控制、Schrödinger桥问题、系统性风险控制和避障路径规划等数值实验上，NFIST展示了有效的零样本泛化能力，同时大幅降低了解大规模随机MFC问题族的计算成本。论文还证明了所提算子学习公式与逐任务优化的相容性。

Conclusion: NFIST开创性地将算子学习范式引入随机MFC领域，解决了扩散项导致的传输映射表达难题，实现了对未见任务族的单次前向求解，兼顾了理论一致性保证和实际计算效率提升。

Abstract: Stochastic mean-field control (MFC) provides a fundamental framework for coordinating large populations of interacting agents under uncertainty, with a wide range of applications. Existing numerical and deep-learning methods solve one MFC problem instance at a time and must be re-optimized whenever the task changes. In this work, we formulate stochastic MFC as an operator-learning problem and develop, to the best of our knowledge, the first mesh-free, self-supervised neural operator for stochastic MFC. The main challenge is that the diffusion term in the controlled Fokker--Planck equation precludes deterministic transport-map representations. We address this challenge by combining the probability-flow ODE with an invertible normalizing-flow-based transformer, which recasts the dynamics as a deterministic continuity equation and enables closed-form score evaluation through the exact inverse and analytical log-determinant of the normalizing flow, with $\mathcal{O}(d)$ cost per particle for networks of fixed size. Through transformer-based in-context learning, task prompts, represented by compact distribution parameters or raw particle clouds, condition the transport map, enabling a single pretrained operator to solve unseen tasks in one forward pass. The resulting \emph{Normalizing Flow Invertible Solution Transformer} (NFIST) is trained end-to-end by minimizing the stochastic control objective directly, requiring no precomputed numerical solutions for training. We further prove the consistency of the proposed operator-learning formulation with task-by-task optimization. Numerical experiments on stochastic optimal control, Schrödinger bridge, systemic-risk control, and obstacle-avoiding path planning demonstrate effective zero-shot generalization while substantially reducing the computational cost of solving large families of stochastic MFC problems.

</details>


### [84] [Anytime Solver Evaluation with a Normalized Signed Primal Integral and Explicit Reference Policies - Extended Version](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18288)
*Florian Rascoussier*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种归一化的有符号基本积分（signed primal integral），用于评估Anytime求解器的进度，该积分基于有界相对间隙，为空运行赋予内在最差值，无需接受阈值，并对超越冻结参考值的incumbent赋予负的瞬时间隙。通过与Berthold的max-normalized gap的有符号版本对比，并在多个实验（路由五臂运动、模型保真度阶梯）上验证，结果表明有符号核保留了所有面板排序，而常见接受阈值会压缩距离甚至翻转一个面板排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Anytime求解器的进度总结方式（如基本积分、最终间隙、收敛曲线）存在多个未明示的选择自由度：如何评分首次可行解之前的运行、是否截断劣质incumbent、参考值是在实验后更新还是版本冻结。这些选择在运行无有效incumbent或改进了已发布最佳已知值时变得不可忽略，可能导致评估失真或排名不一致。

Method: 提出一种归一化的有符号基本积分，基于有界相对间隙（difference-over-sum核函数），为空运行分配固有最差值，不依赖接受阈值，对超越冻结参考值的incumbent分配负瞬时间隙；并与有符号版本的Berthold max-normalized gap对比；区分分析时参考策略与版本冻结参考策略；建议结合原始最终间隙、平均收敛曲线和目标达成曲线共同解读分数；在五个路由臂和两个模型保真度阶梯上系统性评估。

Result: 两种有符号核在研究中保持所有面板排序，而常见接受阈值压缩了臂间距离并翻转了一个面板排序；更新212个参考值中的54个会改变分数水平并消除所有负分数，但面板排序不变；探索性筛选识别出9个面板比较中相似积分分数隐藏了显著不同的端点或达成率。

Conclusion: 有符号归一化基本积分是评估Anytime求解器进度的更稳健、更一致的指标，避免了现有方法的任意性选择；应搭配原始间隙、收敛曲线和目标达成曲线综合解读；推荐在实际分析中优先使用difference-over-sum核函数及版本冻结参考策略。

Abstract: Anytime solvers return usable solutions before they terminate and improve them while time remains. Their progress is commonly summarized by a primal integral, a final gap, or convergence curves. Each summary leaves consequential choices open: how to score a run before its first feasible solution, whether poor incumbents are truncated, and whether the reference value is updated after the experiment or version-frozen beforehand. These choices become visible when runs produce no valid incumbent or improve a published best-known value. We study a normalized signed primal integral built from a bounded relative gap. It assigns an intrinsic worst value to an empty run, requires no acceptance threshold, and assigns negative instantaneous gaps to incumbents that beat a frozen reference. We compare the smooth difference-over-sum kernel with a signed version of Berthold's max-normalized gap and use the former as a working default. We also distinguish analysis-time from version-frozen reference policies and recommend reading the score with the raw final gap, mean convergence curve, and target-attainment curve. We evaluate these choices on a five-arm routing campaign and two model-fidelity ladders. The two signed kernels preserve every panel ordering in this study, whereas a common acceptance threshold compresses the distances between arms and reverses one panel ordering. Updating 54 of the 212 reference values changes score levels and removes all negative scores, while leaving the observed panel orderings unchanged. An exploratory screen also identifies nine panel comparisons in which similar integral scores conceal materially different endpoints or attainment rates. The implementation, frozen inputs, and generators are openly released.

</details>

*Xin Guo,Mehdi Talbi,Qinxin Yan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对有限参与者非零和最优停止博弈，提出了一种独立随机化表述。每个停止规则由适应的非降累积停止过程表示，规范嵌入保持纯策略收益，且原始博弈的纯策略纳什均衡与随机化博弈的纳什均衡等价。通过α-势方法构造α_N势函数，弱相互作用下误差为O(N^{-1})，并识别出具有闭式阈值均衡的精确势子类。对于局部停止状态交互，随机化收益具有局部停止质量表示，势最大化可表述为多维奇异控制问题。文章还提出了未知模型系数下的有界强度Potential-CT-DDPG学习算法，数值实验与解析基准高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限参与者非零和最优停止博弈的均衡系统复杂度随参与者数量快速增长，现有方法难以扩展到大N场景，亟需一种可扩展且保持均衡等价性的表述与求解方法。

Method: (1) 引入独立随机化表述，用适应的非降累积停止过程表示每个停止规则，建立规范嵌入保持纯策略收益和纳什均衡等价性；(2) 采用α-势方法构造α_N-势函数，弱交互下误差为O(N^{-1})；(3) 识别精确势子类并给出闭式阈值均衡；(4) 将势最大化转化为多维奇异控制问题，研究HJB拟变分不等式及其正则性；(5) 提出有界强度Potential-CT-DDPG学习算法处理未知模型系数。

Result: 建立了随机化博弈与原始博弈的纳什均衡等价性；构造了误差O(N^{-1})的α_N-势函数；推导了精确势子类的闭式阈值均衡；对局部相互作用给出了HJB QVI的正则性分析；数值实验与解析基准匹配，估计的最佳响应改进符合N^{-1}标度。

Conclusion: 随机化表述结合α-势方法能够有效缓解有限参与者最优停止博弈的均衡计算复杂度问题，并提供可扩展的学习算法，数值结果验证了理论标度。

Abstract: Finite-player nonzero-sum optimal stopping games typically lead to coupled equilibrium systems whose complexity grows rapidly with the number of players. We introduce an independently randomized formulation in which each stopping rule is represented by an adapted, nondecreasing cumulative stopping process. The canonical embedding preserves pure-profile payoffs, and a pure profile is a Nash equilibrium of the original game if and only if its embedding is a Nash equilibrium of the randomized game. We adopt the $α$-potential approach to construct an $α_N$-potential function, with the error $α_N=O(N^{-1})$ under weak-interaction. We also identify an exact-potential subclass with a closed-form threshold equilibrium. For local stopped-status interactions, randomized payoffs admit a local stopped-mass representation, and potential maximization can be formulated as a multidimensional singular-control problem with local gradient constraints and a nonlocal condition for finite jumps. Under suitable regularity assumptions, we study the associated Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman quasi-variational inequality and its regularity properties. For unknown model coefficients, we propose a bounded-intensity Potential-CT-DDPG learning algorithm. Numerical experiments closely match the analytical benchmark and yield estimated best-response improvements consistent with $N^{-1}$ scaling.

</details>


### [86] [Optimization of the Railcar Assignment Problem Using Zone-based Double Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18442)
*Ruonan Zhao,Joseph Geunes*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本研究针对平面编组站中的铁路车辆分配问题（RAP），提出了一个融合实际运营约束的混合整数规划（MIP）模型并证明了其NP难性。为求解大规模实例，提出了一种基于区域分解的双深度Q网络（Zone-DDQN）启发式方法，将编组站划分为多个平行区域后逐一用DDQN求解。实验表明，Zone-DDQN在小型编组站实例上平均最优性差距为5.71%，在超过150辆铁路车辆和30条轨道的大规模实例中，能在平均214.42秒内求解，而MIP模型在24小时内无法获得解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁路车辆调车操作对编组站的高效运营至关重要，直接影响货物运输的快速有效流动。在平面编组站中，具有预定目的的铁路车辆分散在多条平行轨道上，需要通过调车操作组装和解编列车。现有研究缺乏同时考虑实际运营约束和高效求解大规模实例的方法，因此需要提出新的模型与求解策略。

Method: 本文首先提出一个包含实际运营约束的新型混合整数规划（MIP）模型用于描述铁路车辆分配问题，并证明了该问题的NP难性。随后提出Zone-DDQN启发式方法：采用编组站区域分解策略将编组站划分为多个平行区域，再应用双深度Q网络（DDQN）对各区域逐一、顺序地求解问题。

Result: 在小型编组站实例上，Zone-DDQN启发式方法的平均最优性差距为5.71%。对于包含超过150辆铁路车辆和30条轨道的大型编组站实例，MIP模型无法在24小时内获得解，而Zone-DDQN平均仅需214.42秒即可求解。

Conclusion: Zone-DDQN启发式方法在求解效率和可扩展性上显著优于传统MIP模型，能够有效处理包含实际运营约束的大规模铁路车辆分配问题，为平面编组站运营提供了实用的决策支持工具。

Abstract: Railcar switching, or shunting operations decisions play a significant role in the efficient operation of railyard systems, which are in turn critical to the fast and effective movement of goods. In flat yards, switching operations are primarily performed using locomotives to push and pull railcars in order to assemble and disassemble trains. In such settings, railcars with predefined destinations are located across multiple parallel rail tracks, and must be moved, or switched, in order to form desired outbound trains. This study addresses the Railcar Assignment Problem (RAP) in flat yards with an objective of minimizing the total number of switching movements. We present a novel mixed-integer programming (MIP) model for this problem that incorporates practical operational constraints in rail yards, and demonstrate its NP-hardness. To solve large-scale instances, we propose a comprehensive Zone-based Double Deep Q-Network (Zone-DDQN) heuristic method that integrates railway structure, yard-zone decomposition, and a Double Deep Q-Network (DDQN). The yard-zone decomposition strategy partitions the yard into multiple parallel yard zones, after which the DDQN is applied to solve the problem within each zone individually and sequentially. Computational experiments across small-, medium-, and large-scale yards were conducted on a series of RAP instances. Average results show that the Zone-DDQN heuristic achieves an average optimality gap of $5.71\%$ across small-scale yard instances. For large-scale yard instances containing more than 150 railcars and 30 tracks, the MIP model was not able to obtain solutions within 24 hours. In contrast, the Zone-DDQN heuristic was able to solve these instances with an average running time of 214.42 seconds.

</details>


### [87] [Data-Driven Output Feedback based Analysis and Control for Unknown Discrete-Time Linear System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18452)
*Haoyan Lin,Jie Huang*

TL;DR: 本文针对未知线性离散时间系统，利用数据信息性（data informativity）概念，首先研究了输入矩阵已知时的数据信息性分析与控制设计，发现此时的信息性条件更为宽松，且状态反馈控制器（镇定、死区控制、LQR）计算方法更直接。随后基于参数化观测器，将未知系统的动态输出反馈控制问题转化为输入矩阵已知的辅助系统的状态反馈控制问题，从而可直接利用第一部分结果计算基于输入输出数据的动态输出反馈镇定和死区控制律。此外，还提出了一种渐近逼近原未知系统状态反馈LQR解的动态输出反馈控制律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于数据信息性的动态输出反馈控制器设计条件较为苛刻，限制了数据驱动控制方法在未知线性离散时间系统中的应用。本文旨在放宽这些条件，简化基于输入输出数据计算动态输出反馈控制律的过程。

Method: 本文采用两阶段方法：首先针对输入矩阵已知的未知系统，进行数据信息性分析并设计更直接的状态反馈控制（镇定、死区控制、LQR）；然后基于参数化观测器，将未知系统的动态输出反馈控制问题映射为一个输入矩阵已知的辅助系统的状态反馈控制问题，从而复用第一部分的结论。最后设计一种渐近逼近状态反馈LQR解的动态输出反馈控制律。

Result: 证明了当输入矩阵已知时，数据信息性条件显著放宽；通过辅助系统转换，实现了基于输入输出数据的动态输出反馈镇定和死区控制；并提出了一种能够渐近逼近原始未知系统状态反馈LQR解的动态输出反馈控制律。

Conclusion: 本文通过放宽信息性条件和系统化的问题转换，为基于数据驱动方法设计未知线性离散时间系统的动态输出反馈控制器提供了更简便且实用的解决方案，扩展了数据信息性理论在输出反馈控制中的应用范围。

Abstract: Using the notion of data informativity, the existing results have given conditions under which the data are informative for controller designs for various control problems of unknown linear discrete-time systems, and have developed methods to compute such controllers from data. Nevertheless, the existing conditions for computing a dynamic output feedback control law based on the input and output data are somehow stringent. In this paper, we first focus on developing data informativity analysis and control for an unknown system with a known input matrix. It turns out that the informativity conditions for such a system are much milder and the methods for computing state feedback control laws for stabilization, deadbeat control, and the linear-quadratic regulator (LQR) for such a system are much more straightforward. Further, based on the parameterized observer, we show that the problem of computing a dynamic output feedback control law for an unknown system can be converted to the problem of designing a state feedback control law for an ancillary system whose input matrix is known. Therefore, the results of the first part of this paper can be directly used to compute a dynamic output feedback control law for stabilization and deadbeat control for unknown linear discrete-time systems based on the input and output data. Moreover, we present a dynamic output feedback control law which will asymptotically approach a state feedback LQR solution to the original unknown system.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实金融市场中，投资者面临交易成本且无法连续调整投资组合，只能在不规则（随机）时刻进行再平衡。已有研究多假设连续交易或确定性离散时间，缺乏对随机干预时刻和比例交易成本同时存在时长期风险敏感投资组合问题的系统分析。本文旨在更贴近现实的假设下，建立遍历Bellman方程的可解性理论。

Method: 利用Schauder不动点定理求解遍历Bellman方程，避免了对路径混合条件的依赖；在额外全支持（full-support）和增长条件下证明解的唯一性和唯一连续最大化子；通过渐近分析建立风险厌恶参数趋零时解收敛于风险中性（Kelly）问题；引入二进时间网格对随机干预时刻进行离散近似并证明其收敛性，辅以数值实验验证理论结果。

Result: 在无需混合假设的条件下成功求解了遍历Bellman方程；建立了解的唯一性和唯一连续最大化子的充分条件；证明了风险厌恶趋零时最优解渐近收敛于Kelly（对数效用）投资策略；二进时间网格离散近似随机干预时刻的方案收敛；数值示例验证了理论分析。

Conclusion: 本文为带比例交易成本和随机再平衡机会的长期风险敏感投资问题建立了严谨的理论框架，拓展了Bellman方程可解性的适用范围（无需混合假设），并在理论层面验证了风险敏感策略向风险中性（Kelly）策略的渐近收敛，为实际投资决策提供了理论支持。

Abstract: We study a long-run risk-sensitive portfolio problem with proportional transaction costs in a continuous-time market whose log-prices are given as a Lévy process, and rebalancing is possible only at random moments of investment opportunities. Using a Schauder fixed-point argument, we solve the ergodic Bellman equation without any mixing assumptions. Under additional full-support and growth conditions, the solution to the Bellman equation is unique, with the unique continuous maximiser. We also prove vanishing risk-aversion asymptotics towards the risk-neutral (Kelly) problem and convergence of a dyadic-time-grid approximation of the random intervention moments. Numerical examples illustrate the results.

</details>


### [89] [Modeling Network Congestion under Demand Uncertainty Using Wardrop Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18708)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际交通管理中，出行需求具有波动性，传统模型往往假设需求确定，无法应对需求波动导致的拥堵风险。因此需要在需求不确定条件下识别最坏情况的拥堵场景，以评估网络的可靠性和鲁棒性。

Method: 将问题建模为单领导者多追随者的双层问题，用户遵循用户均衡或系统最优两种Wardrop原则。考虑椭球形、预算型不确定性集合以及hose多面体等不同需求不确定模型。通过引入二进制变量和big-M常数将双层模型转化为单层混合整数非线性规划，推导出有效的大M值并设计多种公式增强技术来强化模型。

Result: 在Sioux Falls网络和SNDlib实例上进行了大量计算实验，验证了所提出模型和增强技术的计算有效性，并分析了不同拥堵度量指标和不确定模型对最坏情况拥堵结果的影响。

Conclusion: 该研究为需求不确定条件下的交通网络最坏情况拥堵评估提供了有效的数学模型和求解技术，为交通管理机构进行网络压力测试和可靠性评估提供了理论工具。

Abstract: Motivated by the need for reliable traffic management under fluctuating travel demand, we study the problem of determining the worst-case congestion in a multi-commodity traffic network subject to demand uncertainty. To this end, we stress-test a given network by identifying demand realizations and corresponding travelers' route choices that maximize congestion. The users of the traffic network are assumed to act according to one of the two Wardrop principles, the user equilibrium or the system optimum, so that the resulting congestion models can be seen as bilevel problems with a single leader and multiple followers. To address uncertain travel demand, we consider different models such as ellipsoidal or budgeted uncertainty sets and the hose polyhedron. We present single-level mixed-integer nonlinear reformulations of the congestion models that exploit binary variables and big-M constants, prove the existence of optimal solutions, derive valid big-Ms, and propose several enhancement techniques to further strengthen the formulations. An extensive computational study on instances of the Sioux Falls network and instances from the SNDlib demonstrates the computational effectiveness of the proposed techniques and provides insight into the impact of different congestion measures and uncertainty models on the resulting worst-case congestion.

</details>


### [90] [An Integer Programming Approach to Compute Lower Bounds for Ramsey Numbers Using Circulant Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18769)
*Stefano Coniglio,Fabio Furini,Ivana Ljubić,Pablo San Segundo,Johannes Thüerauf,Emiliano Traversi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个整数规划框架，用于计算和验证循环图上的Ramsey数下界。通过利用循环图的旋转对称性，将问题投影到距离空间，将二元变量从二次减少到线性。通过系数加强、分支切割算法以及利用循环图公共邻域结构的分离例程（结合启发式和精确最大团算法），在多达410个顶点的循环图上开展了大规模计算实验，将25个R(3,n)值（24≤n≤49且n≠27）的已知下界最多提高了11个点，并首次提供了可复现的优化流程来验证循环Ramsey数R_C(3,n)，确定了13≤n≤20范围内的八个新值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确确定Ramsey数极其困难，而验证下界需要显式构造避免特定团的无冲突着色。传统方法难以获得高质量下界，且缺乏可复现和可独立验证的优化流程。

Method: 提出整数规划框架，将搜索限制在循环图上，利用其旋转对称性在投影距离空间中重构问题，将二元变量数从O(n²)降至O(n)；通过系数加强强化投影模型；采用分支切割算法，其分离例程利用循环图的公共邻域结构，结合启发式与精确最大团算法进行分离。

Result: 在最多410个顶点的循环图上，将25个R(3,n)值（24≤n≤49，n≠27）的已知最佳下界最多提高11个点；首次提供可复现的优化流程用于认证循环Ramsey数R_C(3,n)，确立了13≤n≤20范围内八个新的R_C(3,n)值。所有图证书和独立验证程序均以补充材料形式提供。

Conclusion: 该整数规划框架是一种有效的、可复现的循环图Ramsey数下界认证方法，显著改进了多个R(3,n)的已知下界，并建立了可独立验证的图证书体系，为Ramsey数的计算研究提供了可靠的工具。

Abstract: The Ramsey number $R(m,n)$ is the smallest order at which every red-blue edge coloring of a complete graph must contain a blue clique (a complete subgraph) of size $m$ or a red clique of size $n$. Determining these numbers exactly is extremely hard, and even certifying a lower bound requires exhibiting an explicit coloring that avoids both cliques. We develop an integer programming framework for certifying such lower bounds, restricting the search to circulant graphs, whose rotational symmetry lets us reformulate the problem in a projected distance space, reducing the number of binary variables from quadratic to linear in the graph order. We strengthen this projected model through coefficient reduction and solve it with a branch-and-cut algorithm whose separation routine exploits the common neighborhood structure of circulant graphs, combining heuristic and exact maximum-clique algorithms. In an extensive computational campaign on circulant graphs with up to 410 vertices, we improve the best lower bounds previously obtained by other methods by up to 11 points for 25 values of $R(3,n)$ with $24\le n\le49$ and $n\neq27$, each backed by an explicit graph certificate that can be independently verified with a stand-alone exact clique solver. To the best of our knowledge, our method also provides the first reproducible optimization-based procedure for certifying circulant Ramsey numbers $R_C(m,n)$, which we use to establish eight new values of $R_C(3,n)$ with $13\le n\le20$. Our framework, graph certificates, and stand-alone checker are provided as supplementary material to support independent verification and reuse.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市网络中与学校相关的集中出行是早高峰拥堵的重要来源，现有研究缺乏将学校上学时间调度与通勤者出发时间选择行为耦合的系统级优化框架。

Method: 构建双层优化模型：上层问题联合最小化总出行时间与学校时间表偏离度；下层问题采用基于alpha-beta-gamma偏好的确定性动态多类别用户均衡模型描述通勤者出发时间选择。开发交替迭代算法，上层通过标准数学规划求解器近似求解，下层通过迭代算法获得近似均衡解。

Result: 数值实验表明该方法可显著降低拥堵，刻画了学校时间灵活性与交通效率之间的权衡关系；敏感性分析考察了MFD不确定性和调度偏好的影响。

Conclusion: 所提双层优化框架能够有效缓解学校相关出行造成的早高峰拥堵，为城市学校时间调度提供了理论依据，但需考虑MFD不确定性与通勤者偏好差异的影响。

Abstract: This paper addresses morning commute congestion caused by concentrated school-related trips in urban networks. We propose a bi-level optimization framework for regulating school start times in a multi-region urban network characterized by Macroscopic Fundamental Diagrams (MFDs), explicitly coupling system-level regulation with multi-class user-equilibrium-based departure-time choices. The Upper-Level problem jointly minimizes total time spent and deviations from current school schedules, while the Lower-Level problem models commuter behavior through a deterministic dynamic multi-class user equilibrium formulation incorporating alpha-beta-gamma preferences for travel time, earliness, and lateness costs. To address the computational challenges arising from the bilevel structure, non-convex traffic dynamics, and endogenous demand responses, an iterative algorithm alternating between the Upper- and Lower-Level problems is developed. The Upper-Level problem is approximated through a formulation solvable with standard mathematical programming solvers, while an iterative algorithm provides an approximate solution to the Lower-Level equilibrium problem. Numerical results demonstrate substantial congestion reductions and characterize the trade-off between school start-time flexibility and traffic efficiency. Sensitivity analyses further examine the effects of MFD uncertainty and scheduling preferences.

</details>

*Denilson Menezes,Juan Límaco*

Method: 通过能量估计来处理二次梯度项带来的非线性困难，并利用控制-状态映射的可微性性质来刻画最优控制-状态对。

Result: 在一般假设下证明了最优控制的存在性，并在实际相关的框架下刻画了最优控制-状态对所满足的条件。

Conclusion: 所提出的能量估计与控制-状态映射可微性方法有效克服了二次梯度项带来的分析难点，为拟线性抛物型方程的最优控制问题提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study an optimal control problem for a quasilinear parabolic equation modeling nonlinear heat conduction during heat treatment of metals. Under general assumptions, we prove existence of an optimal control and characterize the associated optimal control-state pair in a practically relevant framework. The main difficulty is the quadratic gradient term, which we handle through energy estimates and differentiability properties of the control-to-state map.

</details>


### [93] [Modeling of an ODE-constrained optimization problem describing tumor dynamics, and numerical approximation via sequential physics-informed neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18974)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胶质母细胞瘤是一种侵袭性极强的脑肿瘤，其生长受氧气（氧浓度）显著影响。为了更有效地设计化疗和抗血管生成疗法的联合治疗方案，需要建立一个考虑氧动力学的最优控制模型，并从理论上保证最优控制的存在性，同时在数值上提供高效可靠的求解方法。

Method: 1) 建立受氧浓度影响的胶质母细胞瘤生长ODE模型，引入化疗和抗血管生成治疗两个控制变量；2) 定义包含肿瘤抑制、氧浓度逼近目标值及治疗代价惩罚的成本泛函；3) 证明最优控制的存在性，并利用Pontryagin最小值原理推导一阶必要条件（伴随系统）；4) 数值求解采用物理信息神经网络（PINNs），将时间域分解为多个子区间，各子区间使用独立神经网络并通过连续性条件衔接（即顺序PINN形式，包括软约束和硬约束版本），配合梯度下降与Armijo线搜索更新控制变量。

Result: 实现了对最优控制问题的求解，数值上证明了顺序PINN方法（软/硬约束版本）能够有效地近似状态和伴随系统，且与经典求解器和传统PINN相比，在时间分解策略下获得了更优或相当的近似结果。

Conclusion: 本文从理论和数值两方面完成了氧影响下胶质母细胞瘤生长模型的最优控制求解，验证了顺序PINN时间分解策略在处理该类最优控制问题的有效性和优势，为肿瘤联合治疗的优化方案提供了可靠的建模与计算框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we study an optimal control problem related to an ODE model of glioblastoma growth influenced by the oxygen. The model considers a couple of controls describing the chemotherapy and antiangiogenic therapies. The cost functional aims to reduce the tumor growth, bring the oxygen concentration close to a desired value, and penalize the use of therapies. We solve the optimal control problem, proving the existence of a global optimal control and deriving first-order necessary optimality conditions through the Pontryagin Minimum Principle. For the numerical approximation, we use Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) to solve the state and adjoint systems, together with a gradient descent method with Armijo line search for the controls. To address the strategy of PINNs we consider the methodology proposed in [15], making a decomposition of the time domain into several subintervals, using different neural networks in each subinterval and enforcing continuity conditions between successive time subintervals. This strategy, called sequential PINN formulations in time, including soft and hard-constrained versions, is compared with the corresponding approximation results of classical solvers and traditional PINNs counterparts.

</details>


### [94] [Robust stabilization of discrete-time linear systems requires nonlinear dynamic feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19010)
*Amir Shakouri,Marieke Heidema,Henk J. van Waarde*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了离散时间线性输入-状态系统的鲁棒镇定问题。证明了对于任何紧致的可镇定系统集合，存在一个动态状态反馈控制器能够全局渐近镇定集合中的所有系统。同时展示了对于某些紧致可镇定系统集合，非线性静态或线性动态状态反馈无法完成任务，从而证明鲁棒镇定通常需要同时具备非线性和动态特性的反馈律。此外还扩展研究了具有给定衰减率的鲁棒指数镇定，并对多面体系统集合提出了鲁棒反馈律设计算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究离散时间线性系统在不确定参数下的鲁棒镇定问题，核心在于确定何种类型的反馈律（静态/动态、线性/非线性）能够保证对所有可镇定系统集合实现全局渐近镇定，这是控制系统理论中的基础性问题。

Method: 通过构造性证明方法，针对紧致可镇定系统集合证明了动态状态反馈控制器的存在性；通过反例构造证明非线性静态和线性动态状态反馈的局限性；对于多面体系统集合，设计了具体的鲁棒反馈律算法。

Result: (1) 任意紧致可镇定系统集合都存在能实现全局渐近镇定的动态状态反馈控制器；(2) 存在某些紧致可镇定系统集合，非线性静态或线性动态状态反馈无法实现全局渐近镇定；(3) 将结果推广到带给定衰减率的鲁棒指数镇定；(4) 针对多面体系统集合给出了鲁棒反馈律的算法设计。

Conclusion: 鲁棒镇定在一般情况下需要同时具有非线性和动态特性的反馈律，单一的非线性静态或线性动态反馈均不够充分。该结论为鲁棒控制器设计提供了重要的理论指导，并为多面体系统集合提供了实用的算法设计方法。

Abstract: This paper studies the problem of robust stabilization of linear input-state systems in discrete time. We prove that for any compact set of stabilizable systems, there exists a dynamic state-feedback controller that globally asymptotically stabilizes all systems in the set. In addition, we show that for some compact sets of stabilizable systems, no nonlinear static or linear dynamic state-feedback law can achieve this task. This proves that, in general, robust stabilization requires a feedback law that is both nonlinear and dynamic. We extend our study to robust exponential stabilization with a given rate of decay. Finally, for polytopic sets of systems, we introduce an algorithm for the design of robust feedback laws.

</details>


### [95] [Constrained Spatial Pricing of On-Street Parking with Bayesian Demand Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19015)
*Ananya Kale,Mohit Apte*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对SFpark等以占用率为目标的街头停车定价策略，利用分层贝叶斯需求模型估计停车价格弹性（后验均值-0.241，95%可信区间[-0.325,-0.176]），并将其后验分布嵌入考虑空间耦合、时间价格变化限制、邻里价格差和软收入下限的约束非线性定价问题中。通过Ipopt求解，与固定弹性方法不同，在联合后验抽取上评估期望损失和CVaR目标。后验CVaR策略在留出场景上的表现优于SFpark阈值规则和历史费率（后验概率1.00）及文献标定的鲁棒策略（概率0.85）。epsilon约束前沿表明后验标定改变了有效定价集，Fillmore、Mission和Marina的60个区域窗口结果展示了占用率带表现、收入与空间差异之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有以占用率为目标的街边停车定价（如SFpark试点）多依赖局部阈值规则，未能考虑空间替代效应及需求响应的不确定性，导致定价策略可能次优。作者希望利用细粒度数据和层次贝叶斯模型，在内生量化需求弹性不确定性后，构建考虑空间耦合的定价优化框架，以提升定价决策的鲁棒性与有效性。

Method: 基于SFpark细粒度数据构建分层贝叶斯需求模型估计停车价格弹性，获取后验分布；将后验分布嵌入具有空间耦合约束（包括时间价格变化限制、邻里价格差、软收入下限）的约束非线性规划中，用Ipopt求解；采用期望损失与CVaR两种目标，在联合后验抽取上评估而非固定单一弹性；通过epsilon约束生成有效定价前沿，并在多区域（Fillmore、Mission、Marina）的60个区域-窗口实例上进行验证。

Result: 后验均价弹性为-0.241（95%可信区间[-0.325,-0.176]）。在留出后验场景中，基于后验的CVaR策略目标值以1.00的后验概率优于SFpark阈值规则和历史费率，以0.85的后验概率优于文献标定的鲁棒策略。epsilon约束前沿显示后验标定改变了有效定价集，多区域实验揭示了占用率带性能、收入与空间差异之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 将不确定性显式纳入贝叶斯估计与空间耦合定价优化，能够系统性地改善基于阈值的经验定价规则，为城市街边停车动态定价提供更具鲁棒性和可解释性的决策支持框架。

Abstract: Occupancy-targeted curb pricing, including San Francisco's SFpark pilot, often relies on local threshold rules that do not account for spatial substitution or uncertainty in demand response. We estimate parking-price elasticities from disaggregated SFpark data using a hierarchical Bayesian demand model and use the resulting posterior distribution in a spatially coupled constrained pricing model. Partial pooling across districts gives a posterior mean elasticity of -0.241 with a 95% credible interval of [-0.325, -0.176]. The pricing problem is formulated as a constrained nonlinear program with spatial coupling, temporal price-change limits, neighbor price gaps, and a soft revenue floor, and is solved with Ipopt. Expected-loss and CVaR objectives are evaluated over joint posterior draws rather than a fixed elasticity. On held-out posterior scenarios, the posterior-based CVaR policy has a lower objective than the SFpark threshold rule and historical tariff with posterior probability 1.00, and than a literature-calibrated robust policy with probability 0.85. Epsilon-constraint frontiers show that posterior calibration changes the efficient pricing set. Results from 60 district-windows across Fillmore, Mission, and Marina also report the associated tradeoffs in occupancy-band performance, revenue, and spatial disparity.

</details>


### [96] [Local null controllability of a quasi-linear system and related numerical experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19023)
*Enrique Fernández-Cara,Juan Límaco,Yuri Thamsten,Denilson Menezes*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩散系数依赖于状态变量梯度的拟线性抛物型系统的零能控问题。在扩散系数的正则性和增长性假设以及正则初始数据条件下，证明了局部零能控性成立，并提出了拟牛顿类迭代算法用于数值计算零控制及对应状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拟线性抛物型系统的零能控问题在理论和应用上都具有重要价值，尤其是当扩散系数依赖于状态梯度时，系统的非线性结构使得经典线性控制理论不再适用，需要发展新的理论工具和数值方法。

Method: 首先考虑线性化系统的零能控问题，推导出控制与状态的新估计；然后利用局部逆定理（Local Inversion Theorem）证明原非线性系统的局部零能控性；最后提出拟牛顿类迭代算法，并将该方法应用于数值近似问题。

Result: 在扩散系数的正则性和增长性假设以及正则初始数据的条件下，证明了局部零能控性成立；所提出的拟牛顿类迭代算法成功计算了零控制及关联状态，并通过多个数值实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究为扩散系数依赖于状态梯度的拟线性抛物型系统提供了局部零能控性的理论保证，同时给出了可行的数值计算框架，兼具理论深度与实际应用价值。

Abstract: This paper concerns the null control of quasi-linear parabolic systems where the diffusion coefficient depends on the gradient of the state variable. In our main theoretical result, with some assumptions on the regularity and growth of the diffusion coefficient and regular initial data, we prove that local null controllability holds. To this purpose, we consider the null controllability problem for the linearized system, we deduce new estimates on the control and the state and, then, we apply a Local Inversion Theorem. We also formulate an iterative algorithm of the quasi-Newton kind for the computation of a null control and an associated state. We apply this method to some numerical approximations of the problem and illustrate the results with several experiments.

</details>


### [97] [A Mini-Batch Counterexample to Last-Iterate Convergence in Definable Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19074)
*Weiwei Kong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对Bolte & Pauwels (2021)中关于小批量随机逼近可定义势函数的收敛猜想（Remark 12）给出了一个反例。通过构造R上两个凸分段仿射（因而半代数）的求和项，并选择满足α_k = o(1/log k)的确定性非增块步长序列，迭代点形成嵌套二进制格点上的惰性反射随机游走，最终几乎必然不收敛但累积点集恰为[-1,1]，且∑α_k²=∞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bolte & Pauwels (2021)在Remark 12中提出了一个关于小批量随机逼近中可定义势函数收敛性的猜想。该猜想如果成立，将扩展可定义优化理论到随机批量场景。本文旨在通过构造具体反例来验证或否定这一猜想，从而厘清该领域的理论边界。

Method: 构造方法包括：(1) 在R上选取两个凸分段仿射（semialgebraic）求和项；(2) 选择确定性非增块步长序列满足α_k = o(1/log k)；(3) 从每个聚合批量场中选取可容许的最小范数选择；(4) 在连续块上，迭代形成嵌套二进制格点上的惰性反射随机游走；(5) 利用显式端点覆盖时间估计、马尔可夫不等式和第一个Borel-Cantelli引理证明几乎必然每个足够晚的块都遍历其整个格点。

Result: 几乎必然地，每个足够晚的块的迭代点都访问其整个格点，从而迭代点保持在[-1,1]内但不收敛，其累积点集恰为[-1,1]，且在该集合上平均目标函数为常数。此外，构造满足∑α_k² = ∞。

Conclusion: 该构造给出了Bolte & Pauwels (2021) Remark 12中收敛猜想的一个反例，表明即使步长满足α_k = o(1/log k)且∑α_k²=∞，可定义势函数的小批量随机逼近也可能不收敛。这推翻了原有猜想，为该领域理论提出了新的限制条件。

Abstract: We give a counterexample to the convergence conjecture in Remark 12 of [Bolte & Pauwels, 2021] for mini-batch stochastic approximation with definable potentials. The construction uses two convex piecewise-affine, hence semialgebraic, summands on $\mathbb{R}$. We choose a deterministic nonincreasing block stepsize sequence satisfying $α_k = o(1/\log k)$ and an admissible minimum-norm selection from each aggregate batch field. On successive blocks, the iterates form lazy reflected random walks on nested dyadic lattices. An explicit endpoint-cover-time estimate, Markov's inequality, and the first Borel-Cantelli lemma imply that almost surely every sufficiently late block's iterates visit their entire lattice. Consequently, the iterates remain in $[-1,1]$ but do not converge, and their accumulation set is exactly $[-1,1]$, on which the averaged objective is constant. Finally, the construction has $\sum_k α_k^2 =\infty$. Both Chat-GPT 5.6 (Sol) and Gemini Pro 3.1 (DeepThink) were used in the development and drafting of this result.

</details>


### [98] [Constrained minmax density transportation for linear parabolic PDEs: a numerical optimal control perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19170)
*Siddhartha Ganguly,Vaibhav Upadhyay,Kenji Kashima,Debasish Chatterjee*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数值最优控制框架，用于解决一类带噪声的线性抛物型偏微分方程（特别是热方程）的极小极大约束密度控制问题。通过有限差分对空间导数离散化，将问题转化为有限参数化的凸半无限规划（SIP），并利用凸SIP理论的新数值工具给出精确解的保证及高效求解算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对带噪声的线性抛物型偏微分方程（如热方程）中的密度控制问题，需要在固定时间范围内将初始密度输运至目标密度，且同时最小化控制代价并最大化扰动影响，同时满足凸路径约束。这类minmax约束控制在真实系统中具有广泛的应用背景，但求解难度大，亟需一个既有理论保证又计算高效的数值框架。

Method: 首先利用有限差分近似对PDE的空间导数进行离散化，将偏微分方程转化为时间上的常微分方程系统；随后对控制和扰动轨迹的可容许空间进行有限参数化，在温和假设下将最优控制问题构造为凸半无限规划（SIP）；最后基于凸SIP理论的新数值工具，建立针对无穷扰动族约束满足的精确解保证，并提出基于优化的高效恢复算法。

Result: 作者给出了在无穷扰动实现族下约束满足的精确解的可验证保证，并提出了一种基于优化的计算高效算法来恢复这些解。文中包含综合数值实验，验证了所提框架的有效性和理论结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为带噪声的线性抛物型PDE的minmax约束密度控制提供了首个具有严格理论保证的凸SIP数值求解框架，兼具解的精确性和计算可行性，为更复杂PDE控制问题提供了可扩展的方法论基础。

Abstract: This article introduces a numerical optimal control framework for minmax constrained density control for a class of noisy linear parabolic partial differential equations (PDEs), in particular the noisy heat equation. The goal is to transport an initial density to a target density while minimizing a specified cost with respect to control actions and maximizing it with respect to disturbances, all within a fixed time horizon while satisfying given convex path constraints. To address this, the spatial derivatives in the PDE are discretized using finite-difference approximations, transforming the problem into a system of ordinary differential equations in time.
