Average-case analysis on random instances has emerged as a powerful framework for understanding typical algorithmic performance beyond worst-case guarantees. A substantial body of work has established negative results: for sufficiently hard instances (often controlled by the underlying graph connectivity/constraints density), no known polynomial-time algorithm can significantly outperform naive heuristics in the double asymptotic limit where both problem size and constraints density tend to infinity. We revisit this picture by studying the finite-size behavior of some optimization algorithms across easy, intermediate, and hard regimes. Through rigorous analysis of large-graph asymptotics combined with numerical experiments on canonical problems (maximum independent set and maximum $K$-SAT), we demonstrate that while algorithms do eventually converge to theoretically predicted bounds, this convergence can be remarkably slow. In the intermediate regime where instances are already highly constrained, local algorithms achieve solutions substantially better than their predicted performance in the high-constraint-density limit. This gap between finite-regime and asymptotic behavior has important practical implications: sophisticated algorithmic design remains crucial even when asymptotic theory predicts inevitable failure.

</details>


### [167] [Converting Expert Deliberation into Financial Signals Through A Context-Aware NLP Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18911)
*Vivek Batra,Kristin Chen,Sanjiv Das,Samuel Judge,Harshad Khadilkar,Sukrit Mittal,Amir Nasrollahzadeh,Daniel Ostrov,Jacob Sisk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CDSP（上下文条件审议信号管道），将投资委员会的会议纪要转化为结构化预测特征，用于预测未来一个月全球股票相对全球债券的表现。在48次月度会议数据集上，模型准确率达到62%-73%，最佳模型（73%准确率、0.73 F1）结合了句子嵌入与CDSP工程特征，但统计上不显著优于始终选择股票（60.4%胜率）的基准。情感极性比提及频率携带更强信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投资委员会会议纪要中包含专家前瞻性信息，但非结构化文本难以直接用于量化预测。现有方法主要依赖原始文本或通用嵌入，缺乏将领域知识（资产类别上下文、财务术语分类）与文本特征工程相结合的框架。本文旨在探索专家审议文本中是否蕴含可提取的市场预测信号。

Method: CDSP管道包含四个步骤：(1) 将会议纪要切分为主题块；(2) 使用大语言模型(LLM)为每个主题块赋予资产类别上下文标签；(3) 将财务关键词映射到预定义的标签分类体系；(4) 构建互补特征——情感极性和提及频率。实验比较了工程特征、原始文本、句子嵌入及组合表示等不同特征设置下的预测性能。

Result: 预测准确率范围为62%-73%，均优于始终选择股票60.4%的基准。最佳模型（73%准确率）结合句子嵌入与CDSP工程特征，取得0.73的F1分数，但性能提升在统计上不显著。情感特征在多个分类类别中比提及频率携带更强信号。

Conclusion: 专家审议文本确实含有前瞻性市场信息，上下文感知的NLP方法（结合LLM特征工程与嵌入表示）有潜力提取这些信号。尽管统计显著性有限，但CDSP管道为将非结构化会议文本转化为可用于资产配置决策的结构化预测特征提供了可行框架，且情感导向的特征比频率类特征更具预测价值。

Abstract: We introduce the CDSP (context-conditional deliberation signal pipeline), converting an investment committee's meeting transcripts into structured predictive features. CDSP segments the meeting transcripts into topical chunks, assigns asset-class context labels using a large language model (LLM), maps financial keywords to a pre-determined taxonomy of labels, and constructs complementary features: sentiment polarity and mention frequency. This feature engineering framework is applied to a dataset spanning 48 monthly committee meetings to predict if global equities will perform better or worse than global bonds in the following month. In experiments with engineered features, raw transcript text, sentence embeddings, and combined representations, the prediction accuracy ranges from 62% to 73%, compared to always choosing stocks, which outperforms bonds 60.4% of the time. The best (73% accurate) model combines sentence embeddings with engineered CDSP features, achieving a 0.73 F1 score (although this is not statistically significant compared to always choosing stocks). Sentiment carries a stronger signal than mention frequency for several taxonomy categories. These findings suggest that experts' deliberations may contain forward-looking information that context-aware NLP can extract.

</details>


### [168] [Score the Algebra, Not the Span: Dimension Reduction for Transfer Operator Models of Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18918)
*Mark Kozdoba,Shie Mannor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"线性掩蔽"（linear masking）现象：对于由多个弱耦合组件组成的动力系统，传统的基于秩（rank-based）的Koopman算子谱分析方法可能需要指数数量的模态才能表示该系统，或者完全丢失某个组件。作者提出基于σ-代数打分的替代准则（χ²散度），使乘积和幂运算"免费"，从而用两倍于动力学内在维数的坐标即可携带算子的完整谱。该方法在基准系统上显著优于VAMP等秩基方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力系统降维的标准做法是对转移（Koopman）算子做谱分解。然而在由多个弱耦合组件组成的系统（物理和生物场景中很常见）中，秩基方法存在根本性缺陷：要么需要指数数量的模态，要么会完全丢失某个组件——该组件在模型中完全缺失而非被粗略建模，导致无法以任何精度预测该组件的任何函数。作者将其称为"线性掩蔽"。

Method: 作者提出不再按秩打分，而是对坐标生成的σ-代数打分。关键思想是：σ-代数允许乘积和幂运算"免费"（不增加维度成本），因此一个组件的代价仅由其生成元决定，而非其所有交互。所提出的准则是嵌入的现在与未来之间的χ²散度，具有预算保证：两倍内在维度的坐标就足以构造一个代数嵌入，其代数携带算子的整个谱（含全部无穷秩）。该准则在变分形式下可借助现成估计器求解；将评判器限制在双线性类中可退化为VAMP分数，因此秩基方法是该族的一个特例。

Result: 在由已发表基准系统组成的复合系统上，秩基方法在所有秩k<100时完全错失被掩蔽的组件，而仅用10个代数坐标即可恢复全部组件。此外，代数表示支持从少量标签预测被掩蔽的组件，而直接从高维观测或VAMP特征进行回归则失败。

Conclusion: 秩基谱方法在弱耦合多组件系统中存在根本性局限（线性掩蔽），基于σ-代数的χ²散度准则能克服这一局限，以更少的坐标捕获完整的算子谱，并在少量标签预测任务上表现出显著优势。该工作统一了秩基方法（作为特例）并提供了更强的理论保证。

Abstract: Dimension reduction for dynamical systems is standard practice, and the standard route is spectral: model the transfer (Koopman) operator by its leading modes. We show that on systems assembled from several weakly interacting components --- a structure common in physical and biological settings --- this may either require an exponential number of modes, or drop an entire component: the component is absent from the model rather than modeled coarsely, and no function of it can be predicted at any accuracy. We call this linear masking.
  The cause is that a rank-based model pays one coordinate per mode. We propose to score instead the $σ$-algebra the coordinates generate, so that products and powers come free and a component's cost is governed only by its generators rather than by all its interactions. The criterion is a $χ^2$-divergence between the embedded present and future, and it carries a budget guarantee: twice the intrinsic dimension of the dynamics is enough coordinates for an embedding whose algebra carries the operator's entire spectrum, with its full infinite rank.
  In variational form the criterion admits off-the-shelf estimators, and restricting its critic to the bilinear class returns the VAMP score on the span, so rank-based methods are one end of the same family. We demonstrate the proposed objective on a composite of published benchmark systems. We exhibit examples where the rank-based methods completely miss the masked components at all ranks $k<100$, while ten algebra coordinates recover all of them. In addition, the resulting algebra representation supports predicting the masked components from few labels, while direct regression from the high-dimensional observation or from the VAMP features fail.

</details>


### [169] [Lost in Aggregation: How Benchmarks Overlook Irreplaceable Model Strengths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18919)
*Andrej Tschalzev,Stefan Lüdtke,Heiner Stuckenschmidt,Christian Bartelt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在表格机器学习基准评测中，除了传统的聚合指标（平均分数、排名等）外，还应考虑数据集层面的"峰值性能前沿"（peak performance frontier），即每个数据集上统计上可获得的最佳性能。通过该框架，模型可被分类为不可替代、充分、冗余或易错。在TabArena基准上的分析表明，常见聚合指标高度相关，主要衡量一致性和避免失败，与数据集层面的不可替代性对齐度低，导致"万金油"型模型被高估，而具有独特数据集优势的模型被低估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基准评测通过聚合指标（均值、排名、成对胜率）评估模型，这类方法适合选择稳健的默认模型，但无法回答一个重要问题：哪些模型是达到特定数据集峰值性能所必需的。现有评测体系可能掩盖模型在特定数据集上的独特价值。

Method: 提出数据中心的峰值性能前沿框架，定义每个数据集上统计上支持的最佳性能，并根据模型在最优前沿上的位置将其分类为不可替代（irreplaceable）、充分（sufficient）、冗余（redundant）或易错（fallible）。将该框架应用于TabArena基准，并分析聚合指标与数据集级不可替代性之间的关系。

Result: 发现常见聚合指标彼此高度相关，主要反映模型的一致性和避免失败的能力，但与数据集级不可替代性的对齐程度较低。表现均衡但从未成为最佳选择的模型在聚合指标中得分较高，而具有独特数据集专项优势的模型在聚合视角下表现平庸。

Conclusion: 基准评估不能仅关注聚合指标的提升，还应当衡量新模型是否扩展了跨数据集的峰值性能可达集合。评测进步应包括模型是否拓展了可实现的最优性能边界。

Abstract: Tabular machine learning benchmarks typically summarize performance by averaging scores, ranks, or pairwise wins across datasets. Such aggregates are useful for selecting robust default models, but they can obscure a different question: which models are necessary to attain peak performance on particular datasets? We argue that benchmark evaluation should also consider the data-centric peak performance frontier, defined by the best statistically supported performance achieved on each dataset. From this perspective, a model may be irreplaceable, sufficient, redundant, or fallible depending on where it lies on the frontier relative to other models. Applying this framework to the TabArena benchmark, we find that common aggregation metrics are highly correlated and largely measure consistency and avoiding failures, while being much less aligned with dataset-level irreplaceability. Consequently, models performing decently across datasets without ever being the best choice are rewarded while models with unique dataset-specific strengths appear mediocre under aggregation. Hence, benchmark progress should be measured not only by improvements on aggregation metrics but also by whether new models expand the set of attainable peak performances across datasets.

</details>


### [170] [Transportable Causal Effect Estimation across Networks under Interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18932)
*Xiaojing Du,Jiuyong Li,Lin Liu,Debo Cheng,Jixue Liu,Thuc Duy Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TranCE算法，首次解决网络干扰下因果效应在不同网络种群之间运输（迁移）的问题。通过扩展选择图到网络场景，区分协变量偏移和结构网络偏移，推导出直接效应、溢出效应和总效应的运输公式，并实现为双稳健算法，在多个基准和真实野外实验上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络干扰下的因果推断方法假设训练网络与部署网络一致，但实际中干预在一个人群上运行，而研究问题涉及另一个人群，两者在拓扑、节点协变量组成和溢出路径上均存在差异。这是一个尚无现有算法解决的数据融合问题。

Method: 1) 将选择图扩展至网络设置，使用独立的选择子分别刻画协变量偏移和结构网络偏移；2) 从扩展选择图中推导部署人群中直接效应、溢出效应和总效应的运输公式，明确说明每个公式假设何种干预机制不变以及需对何种观测分布进行重加权；3) 将公式实现为TranCE算法——一个结合干预结果模型、域密度比校正和交叉拟合推断的双稳健算法。

Result: 在两个基于真实社交网络构建的半合成基准以及一个完全真实的天气保险野外实验中，将运输后的效应与留出随机化估计进行对照，结果验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: TranCE能够有效实现跨网络种群的因果效应运输，有望改进网络化系统（尤其是社交网络和公共卫生领域）中的干预策略。

Abstract: Estimating causal effects under network interference typically assumes that the network used for training and the network used for deployment coincide. In practice, an intervention is run on one population while the question of interest concerns a different population, and the two generally differ in topology, node-covariate composition, and spillover pathways. Transporting a causal effect across networks is therefore a data-fusion problem that no existing algorithm solves. We employ a selection diagram, extended to the network setting so that covariate shift and structural network shift enter as separate selectors, and derive from it a transport formula for the direct, spillover, and total effects in the deployment population. Each formula makes explicit which interventional mechanism is assumed invariant and which observational distribution must be reweighted. We then turn the formulas into TranCE (Transported Causal Effects), a doubly-robust algorithm combining an interventional outcome model, a domain density-ratio correction, and cross-fitted inference. Extensive experiments on two semi-synthetic benchmarks derived from real-world social networks and on a fully real weather-insurance field experiment, where the transported effects are checked against held-out randomized estimates, confirm the effectiveness of our approach. Our findings have the potential to improve intervention strategies in networked systems, particularly in social networks and public health.

</details>


### [171] [Graphical Design of Interpretable Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18936)
*Pietro Barbiero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Penrose张量记号扩展的图形化表示法，用于设计、实现和比较可解释AI架构。该表示法既能从全局视角呈现架构，又能与PyTorch einsum代码一一对应，弥补了符号方程（无全局视图）和概率图模型/流程图（不描述实际张量运算）的不足。作者用该记号描述了多种天然可解释的架构（如概念瓶颈、稀疏探针、原型网络、神经加法模型和线性模型混合），并进一步分析了前沿可解释语言模型Steerling-8B的架构，揭示其为残差模型，并能直接翻译为33行PyTorch代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可解释架构表示方法存在两难困境：符号方程缺乏全局概览，而概率图模型和流程图虽然给出全局视图却不描述实际的张量操作，掩盖了关键洞察并限制了可复现性。需要一个既能提供全局视角又能精确描述张量运算的形式化表示语言。

Method: 从Penrose张量记号出发进行适配和扩展，提出一种图形化记号系统。该记号满足两个核心要求：(1) 在全局层面呈现架构的整体结构；(2) 与PyTorch einsum代码建立一一对应关系，确保精度和可复现性。作者用该记号形式化描述了概念瓶颈、稀疏探针、原型网络、神经加法模型和线性模型混合等可解释架构，并用于刻画Steerling-8B的关键架构组件。

Result: 该图形记号成功描述了多种可解释架构，并应用于Steerling-8B的分析：图示揭示了其残差模型的本质，为每个单独操作提供了几何解释，并直接转化为33行PyTorch代码。

Conclusion: 该图形化表示法弥合了全局视图与精确实现在表示上的鸿沟，为设计、实现和比较可解释AI架构提供了一种正式且可复现的统一语言框架。

Abstract: Designing, implementing, and comparing interpretable architectures requires a formal language to represent them. The most common representations fall short in one of two ways. Symbolic equations give no global view of an architecture at a glance. Probabilistic graphical models and flowcharts do not describe actual tensor manipulations, thus hiding key insights and limiting reproducibility. To close this gap, we introduce a graphical notation for designing interpretable AI architectures, adapted from Penrose tensor notation. This graphical notation gives a global view of an architecture and maps one to one onto PyTorch einsum code. We first use this notation to describe architectures that are interpretable by construction, including concept bottlenecks, sparse probes, prototype networks, neural additive models, and mixtures of linear models. We then diagram the key architectural components of Steerling-8B, a frontier interpretable language model. The diagram yields global insights into the architecture (e.g., showing that Steerling is a residual model), a geometric interpretation of each individual operation, and a direct translation into 33 lines of PyTorch code.

</details>


### [172] [Training Chemical Plausibility-Aware Large Language Models for Single-Step Retrosynthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18940)
*Bogdan Zagribelnyy,Ivan Ilin,Nikita Bondarev,Maksim Kuznetsov,Mathieu Reymond,Vladimir Aladinskiy,Alex Aliper,Alex Zhavoronkov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Top-K提示范式用于单步逆合成预测，构建了包含约4560万条验证反应的大规模数据集CREED-CCV-2+USPTO-XL，训练了化学约束一致语言模型C3LM，结合ChemCensor与新颖性导向奖励进行微调，在OOD URSA-expert-2026基准上达到SOTA性能，并发现LLM与传统模型探索互补反应空间，支持集成式逆合成系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单步逆合成是计算机辅助合成规划的核心，但其内在的一对多（one-to-many）特性未被现有的单一答案评估和基准测试协议充分捕获，需要更稳健的训练与推理范式来应对。

Method: 1) 提出Top-K提示（Top-K prompting）作为训练与推理范式；2) 构建超大规模数据集CREED-CCV-2+USPTO-XL（约4560万条验证反应）；3) 训练C3LM（化学约束一致语言模型）；4) 集成ChemCensor（化学约束过滤）与新颖性导向奖励进行微调。

Result: 在OOD URSA-expert-2026基准上达到SOTA性能；反应唯一性分析表明LLM与传统模型探索互补的反应空间，支持基于集成的逆合成系统。

Conclusion: Top-K、可感知合理性的训练范式为未来稳健的基于LLM的合成规划提供了实用新方向。

Abstract: Single-step retrosynthesis is a central component of computer-aided synthesis planning, yet its intrinsically one-to-many nature is poorly captured by single-answer evaluation and benchmarking protocols. To address this, we introduce Top-K prompting as a robust training and inference paradigm to better capture diverse, plausible reaction predictions. We compile CREED-CCV-2+USPTO-XL, an ultra-large-scale dataset of ~45.6 million verified reactions to train the C3LM (Chemistry Constraint-Consistent Language Model). By integrating fine-tuning with ChemCensor-based and novelty-oriented rewards, our model achieves state-of-the-art performance on the OOD URSA-expert-2026 benchmark. Further analysis of reaction uniqueness shows that LLMs and conventional models explore complementary reaction spaces, motivating ensemble-based retrosynthesis systems. Overall, our results establish Top-K, plausibility-aware training as a practical new direction for robust future LLM-based synthesis planning.

</details>


### [173] [Fuzzy Accuracy Compensates for Label Subjectivity in Classification of Skin Tone Using Wearable Photoplethysmography Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18969)
*Padmini Krishnadas,Urs Hackstein,Alen Bosnjakovic,Philip J. Aston*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究使用光电容积脉搏波（PPG）信号对六类Fitzpatrick肤色进行序数分类。由于标签存在主观性误差，传统准确率仅40-55%，但通过引入"模糊准确率"（预测与标注类别相差不超过1类即视为正确），准确率可提升至96%。研究比较了三种机器学习方法，发现基于原始PPG信号使用模糊交叉熵损失的深度学习/树模型效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肤色标签由人工对照色卡主观判定，含有广泛的小尺度标注误差，导致传统准确率指标（40-55%）无法真实反映模型性能。作者希望通过引入模糊准确率这一更合理的评估指标，证明PPG信号中确实包含可辨识的肤色信息。

Method: 采用三种机器学习方法：（1）对原始PPG信号使用深度学习或树基模型，并引入模糊交叉熵损失函数；（2）通过对称投影吸引子重构（SPAR）方法将信号转化为图像表示，再用深度学习处理；（3）对从信号中提取的特征使用机器学习。评估指标除传统准确率外，引入"模糊准确率"（预测与标注类别相差不超过一个类别即算正确）。

Result: 树基模型在原始信号上取得最高55%的传统准确率和96%的模糊准确率；深度学习模型在SPAR图像上取得44%准确率和85%模糊准确率；基于PPG特征的机器学习取得42%准确率和87%模糊准确率。树基模型配合模糊交叉熵损失的方法（结合第一种方法）效果最佳。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在考虑标签主观误差的模糊评估体系下，PPG信号能够以高模糊准确率预测肤色类别（误差不超过一个类别），证明PPG信号受肤色影响且存在可辨识的差异，为后续肤色相关生理信号研究提供了新评估框架和建模思路。

Abstract: We consider the problem of classification of skin tone using photoplethysmography (PPG) signals with labels of the ordinal six-class Fitzpatrick skin tones. A typical accuracy for this task is a poor 40-55 %. However, the labels are subjectively determined by comparing the skin with a colour chart, and hence contain widespread small-scale inaccuracies. By working with a "fuzzy accuracy", which deems a prediction of skin tone class to be correct if its difference from the labelled class is not greater than one, much higher accuracy is obtained which provides more convincing evidence that skin tone can be accurately predicted from PPG signals. Three machine learning approaches were used, namely deep learning or tree-based approaches on raw PPG signals, deep learning on image representations of the signals generated by the Symmetric Projection Attractor Reconstruction (SPAR) method, and machine learning on features extracted from the signals. The first method also employed a fuzzy version of the cross entropy loss function, which gave the best results. Tree-based models on raw signals give accuracies up to 55 % and higher fuzzy accuracies up to 96 %, while deep learning models on the SPAR images obtained lower results of 44 % accuracy and 85 % fuzzy accuracy. The machine learning on PPG features gave similar results to the SPAR method with accuracy of 42 % and fuzzy accuracy of 87 %. We have shown that classification of skin tone using PPG signals is possible with high fuzzy accuracy which implies that our modelling approach enables accurate prediction of skin tone class within at most one class of the observer's choice of class, from which we conclude that PPG signals are affected by skin tone in a discernible way.

</details>


### [174] [Monroe: A Molecular Foundation Model for In-Context Probabilistic Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18982)
*Blazej Banaszewski,Andrew W. Fitzgibbon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Monroe，一种新型分子基础模型（MFM），通过在8160万分子上进行大规模预训练、改进立体化学图表示、优化训练损失（conformer去噪和嵌入解相关）、改进多任务学习，并使用TabPFN进行下游上下文预测，在Polaris基准和活性悬崖基准上优于或持平现有方法，且PFN策略可迁移到MiniMol和CheMeleon模型上，产生新的SOTA变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物发现的生物活性预测常受限于数据稀缺，因为湿实验耗时且昂贵。现有分子基础模型（MFM）旨在编码通用化学知识以在数据受限场景下泛化，但仍有提升空间。作者希望通过扩大预训练规模、改进化学表示和训练策略，以及引入新的下游预测范式来解决这一问题。

Method: Monroe的创新包括：(1) 扩大的预训练规模——在来自PM6量子化学数据集的8100余万分子上预训练；(2) 改进立体化学的图表示；(3) 改进训练损失，包括conformer去噪和嵌入解相关；(4) 改进多任务学习；(5) 使用prior-data-fitted模型（TabPFN）进行下游上下文预测。评估采用成对比较框架来度量统计显著的性能差异。

Result: 在Polaris基准上，Monroe匹配或超过现有MFM；在活性悬崖（activity cliff）基准上取得显著改进。消融和迁移实验表明，PFN下游预测器也显著改进了MiniMol和CheMeleon，产生新的SOTA变体MiniMol_PFN和CheMeleon_PFN，证明该策略具有泛化性。

Conclusion: Monroe在数据受限的生物活性预测场景中展示了MFM的能力提升，且其PFN下游适配策略具有模型通用性，超越了Monroe本身，为分子基础模型的下游预测提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Bioassay activity prediction is often data-limited because drug-discovery datasets rely on time-consuming and expensive wet-lab experiments for data generation and evaluation. This challenge has inspired recent research into molecular foundation models (MFMs), which aim to encode general-purpose chemical knowledge into molecular representations that generalize well in data-constrained scenarios. This paper presents Monroe, a new MFM with several innovations over the existing state of the art: increased scale allowing pre-training on over 81 million molecules from the PM6 quantum chemistry dataset; improved graph representation of stereochemistry; improved training losses including conformer denoising and embedding decorrelation; improved multi-task learning; and the use of a prior-data-fitted model (TabPFN) for downstream in-context prediction. Our evaluations use a principled pairwise comparison framework that measures statistically significant performance differences. Across established Polaris benchmarks, Monroe matches or exceeds existing MFMs, while on activity cliff benchmarks, designed to assess utility for molecular discovery, it achieves significant improvements over prior methods. Finally, ablation and transfer experiments show that PFN-based downstream predictors also substantially improve two leading existing models, MiniMol and CheMeleon, yielding new state-of-the-art variants we call MiniMol_PFN and CheMeleon_PFN, suggesting that our downstream adaptation strategy generalizes beyond Monroe. Source code is at github.com/blazejba/monroe.

</details>


### [175] [Harness Continual Learning: Continual Adaptation Beyond Model Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19013)
*Borui Kang,Jinrui Gu,Junhan Lv,Wenbin Li,Lei Wang,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"Harness Continual Learning (HCL)"新范式，在冻结基础模型之上，通过持续演化外部"harness"（提示、记忆、工具、技能、路由规则等）来提升智能体能力，并引入"guarded harness evolution"机制通过候选生成与评估分离来实现能力积累与故障恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统持续学习以模型参数为中心，但现代智能体还可通过提示、记忆、工具、技能和路由规则等外部harness来适应。由于这些内容共同影响后续执行，更新harness可能破坏之前可靠的行为——即即便模型冻结，仍存在"harness级遗忘"问题。这一问题尚未被系统化研究，因此需要一个新范式来回答：智能体如何在更新模型外部状态的同时保留已获得的行为能力。

Method: 定义HCL范式，将harness围绕冻结的基础模型持续演化。具体包含：(1) 四个执行面向的组件：任务接口（Task Interface）、经验记忆（Experience Memory）、能力图谱（Capability Map）和自适应路由器（Adaptive Router）；(2) 引入"受保护的harness演化"（guarded harness evolution），将更新生成与状态提交分离——持续优化器（Continual Optimizer）从执行后反馈中提出候选harness，持续评估器（Continual Evaluator）仅在验证当前改进、历史保留和有效性后才提交候选harness。

Result: 在文本推理、多模态感知和开放世界交互三类任务上的实验表明，所提方法实现了能力积累和故障恢复，在多种设置下相较基线相对提升超过10%。组件消融实验评估了每个harness组件的贡献；受控保留扫描实验揭示了可测量的harness级遗忘，并证明稳定性-可塑性权衡可以被显式调节。

Conclusion: HCL为模型外部状态的持续学习提供了系统化的范式框架和实现机制，验证了harness级遗忘的存在，并展示了一种通过"生成-评估"分离来控制稳定性-可塑性权衡的可行路径，扩展了持续学习的研究范围。

Abstract: Continual learning has largely been model-centric, treating model parameters as the state that changes with sequential experience. Modern agents can also adapt through a harness of prompts, memories, tools, skills, and routing rules. Because these contents jointly shape later execution, a harness update can disrupt previously reliable behavior even when the model is frozen. This raises a new question: how can an agent continually improve its state outside the model while retaining behavior acquired earlier? We formulate Harness Continual Learning (HCL), a new continual learning paradigm in which the harness evolves around a frozen foundation model, and define the resulting loss of earlier behavior as harness-level forgetting. We instantiate HCL with four execution-facing components: the Task Interface, Experience Memory, Capability Map, and Adaptive Router. We further introduce guarded harness evolution to separate update generation from state commitment. A Continual Optimizer proposes candidate harnesses from post-execution feedback, and a Continual Evaluator commits the resulting candidate harness only after checking current improvement, historical retention, and validity. Experiments on textual reasoning, multimodal perception, and open-world interaction demonstrate capability accumulation and failure recovery, with relative gains exceeding 10% over corresponding baselines in multiple settings. Component ablations assess the contribution of each harness component, while controlled retention sweeps reveal measurable harness-level forgetting and show that the stability--plasticity trade-off can be explicitly adjusted.

</details>


### [176] [Multi-Agent Off-Policy Deep Reinforcement Learning for Smart Campus Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19049)
*Omar Rady,Mohamed Ayman,Ali Arafa,Mohamed Shalma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究毫米波基站（mmWave BS）在非凸校园拓扑中的最优部署问题，将该NP-hard优化问题建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），并系统对比了四种深度强化学习（DRL）方案：离散单智能体DQN、空间划分多智能体DQN、连续单智能体DDPG和地理划分多智能体DDPG。实验表明多智能体DDPG在密集场景中显著优于单智能体方法，实现了全覆盖及0.94的Jain公平性指数，并在400用户的密集场景中展现出高效的计算收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波基站的部署优化是NP-hard问题，由于max-min公平性目标的非凸、非光滑特性，传统优化方法难以有效求解。深度强化学习因其实时适应性和处理复杂优化问题的能力，为这一问题提供了新的解决思路，因此需要系统研究不同DRL方案在该场景下的可行性和性能差异。

Method: 将基站放置问题建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），并基准测试四种深度强化学习方案：离散单智能体Deep Q-Network（DQN）、空间划分的多智能体DQN、连续单智能体Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient（DDPG）以及地理划分的多智能体DDPG框架，在现实非凸校园拓扑中进行系统对比评估。

Result: 数值评估结果表明：多智能体DDPG方法在密集场景中大幅优于单智能体方法；实现了全覆盖（full coverage）；获得了0.94的Jain公平性指数；多智能体方法在400用户的密集场景中展现出极高的计算收敛效率。

Conclusion: 多智能体DDPG框架是解决毫米波基站部署这一NP-hard优化问题的有效方案，尤其在密集用户场景下表现优越，兼具覆盖完整性和公平性保障，同时具有高效的计算收敛特性，为实际校园场景中的毫米波基站部署提供了可行且性能优异的DRL解决方案。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) has recently gained a great attention due to its real-time adaptation and effectiveness in complex optimization problems. This paper investigates the optimal deployment of millimeter-wave (mmWave) base stations (BSs) in a realistic, non-convex campus topology. The optimization problem is NP-hard, due to the non-convex, non-smooth nature of the max-min fairness objective. To overcome these constraints, we formulate the BS placement as a Markov Decision Process (MDP) and systematically benchmark four DRL schemes: a discrete single-agent Deep Q-Network (DQN), a spatially partitioned Multi-Agent DQN, a continuous single-agent Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (DDPG), and a geographically partitioned multi-agent DDPG framework. Numerical evaluations reveal that the multi-agent DDPG approach substantially outperforms single-agent in dense scenarios. Additionally full coverage is achieved, and a fairness Jain's index of 0.94 is obtained. Finally, the multi-agent demonstrates highly efficient computational convergence of dense scenarios with $400$ users.

</details>


### [177] [Does Mapping Non-Maximal Probabilities to GMM Components Matter for S-JEPA Encoder Representations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19084)
*Wenxuan He,Yunpeng Li,Shan Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过两组匹配对照实验证明，S-JEPA中软高斯混合模型(GMM)目标不仅数值概率重要，非最大概率值分配给哪些GMM分量也影响编码器学习到的表征。若将非最大概率重新映射到不同分量或均匀分布，模型性能均下降，说明概率结构与分量映射共同决定学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: S-JEPA使用软GMM后验而非硬聚类标签以保留不确定性，但现有研究未厘清一个问题：仅依赖概率值本身是否足够，还是说非最大概率分配给哪些GMM分量同样重要。该问题对理解软目标的表征学习机制至关重要。

Method: 设计两组匹配对照实验：FIXED-RANDPERM保留顶层分量及其概率和非最大概率值集合，但按每个物理帧固定的映射重新分配这些值；UNIFORM-TAIL保留顶层分量、其概率及非最大概率总质量，但将该质量均匀分布。在三种独立随机种子和两个冻结Encoder读数上比较三种方法，并开展两组暴露实验及对切换在线GMM后Phase 2轨迹的定性追踪。

Result: REAL SOFT在两个冻结Encoder读数上均优于两个对照组，更好恢复原始GMM尾部，且控制当前帧完整频谱后，对短时间尺度光谱动力学具有更高的可及性。两组暴露实验中，保留原始映射的帧越多，两个读数整体表现越好。

Conclusion: 软目标的数值概率结构并不能完全决定编码器学习到的表征。非最大概率值在GMM分量间的映射关系同样具有关键作用，这为理解软损失目标在自监督学习中的信息编码方式提供了新的视角。

Abstract: S-JEPA uses soft Gaussian mixture model (GMM) posteriors instead of hard cluster labels to preserve uncertainty. It remains unclear whether the probability values alone are sufficient, or whether it also matters which GMM components receive the non-maximal probabilities. We test this with two matched controls. FIXED-RANDPERM keeps the top-1 component and probability together with the multiset of non-maximal probability values, but reassigns those non-maximal values using a mapping fixed for each physical frame. UNIFORM-TAIL keeps the top-1 component, its probability, and total non-maximal mass but distributes that mass uniformly. Across three independent seeds, REAL SOFT outperforms both controls on two frozen Encoder readouts. It provides better recovery of the original GMM tail and greater accessibility of spectral dynamics over short time scales after controlling for the complete spectrum of the current frame. In two exposure experiments, both readouts improved overall as more frames retained the original mapping. We also descriptively follow one Phase 2 trajectory after the switch to the online GMM. These results show that the numerical probability structure of the soft target does not fully determine the learned Encoder representation. The mapping of non-maximal probabilities to GMM components also matters.

</details>


### [178] [Open-MOPD: Diagnosing and Fixing Capability Imbalance in Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19098)
*Huan-ang Gao,Haohan Chi,Yong Yan,Shiyuan Feng,Hanlin Wu,Zheng Jiang,Bingxiang He,Wei-Ying Ma,Ya-Qin Zhang,Hao Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了多教师在线蒸馏（M-OPD）的可复现基准，揭示了标准M-OPD仅能恢复35.6%的性能上限（相对领域路由的oracle集成），其失败根源并非梯度冲突而是token级优化预算的严重错配。作者提出Open-MOPD框架，通过token份额平衡、瓶颈感知的动态预算分配和学生奖励刷新三个机制，将性能恢复提升至83.4%，并完全开源后训练配方与评估套件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多教师在线蒸馏（M-OPD）在实际应用中虽有成效，但其跨领域能力整合的优化动力学机制尚不清晰，且缺乏开放、严格可复现的基准。已有方法无法解释标准M-OPD为何在能力整合上存在显著差距，也无法提供系统性的解决方案。

Method: 首先在SmolLM3-3B-Base上构建受控M-OPD基准（使用oracle路由以隔离能力整合与路由歧义），通过系统分析识别出三个导致优化预算错配的因素：（1）跨领域的序列长度结构性差异；（2）非均匀学习率导致的动态收敛漂移；（3）异步策略更新导致的多步奖励陈旧性。基于此提出Open-MOPD框架，包含token份额平衡、gap感知的动态预算分配和学生奖励刷新三个核心机制。

Result: 标准M-OPD仅捕获35.6%的相对领域路由oracle集成的性能余量，其中如指令跟随等简洁任务严重退化并过早停滞。Open-MOPD通过三个机制系统性地恢复跨领域平衡，将余量恢复从35.6%提升至83.4%，且仅需一个可部署的学生模型。全部代码、训练轨迹和评估套件已开源，可在学术级硬件预算上运行。

Conclusion: M-OPD的能力整合失败根源是token级优化预算错配而非梯度冲突，通过系统性的预算重新平衡机制可以显著恢复跨领域能力整合效果。该工作为M-OPD提供了首个受控基准和开放可复现的端到端后训练配方，具有重要的实践与学术价值。

Abstract: Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (M-OPD) has emerged as a promising paradigm for consolidating domain-specialized reinforcement learning (RL) experts into a single generalist student via dense, token-level reward supervision. Despite its practical success, the optimization dynamics governing multi-teacher capability integration remain poorly understood, and open, rigorously reproducible recipes are conspicuously lacking. In this work, we establish a controlled M-OPD benchmark on SmolLM3-3B-Base with oracle routing, isolating capability integration from routing ambiguity. Our investigation reveals a pronounced capability integration gap: standard M-OPD captures only 35.6% of the available headroom relative to a domain-routed oracle ensemble, with concise tasks such as instruction following suffering severe degradation and premature stagnation. Crucially, we show that this failure stems not from gradient conflict, but from a severe misallocation of the token-level optimization budget. This pathology is driven by three orthogonal factors: structural sequence-length disparities across domains, dynamic convergence drift due to non-uniform learning rates, and multi-step reward staleness from asynchronous policy updates. To resolve these imbalances, we introduce Open-MOPD, a principled framework incorporating token-share balancing, gap-aware dynamic budget allocation, and student reward refresh. Together, these mechanisms systematically restore cross-domain balance, elevating headroom recovery from 35.6% to 83.4% in a single deployable student. We fully open-source our end-to-end post-training recipe, training trajectories, and evaluation suites on an academically accessible hardware budget.

</details>


### [179] [Pretraining Reusable Inference Across Views with Synthetic Task Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19115)
*Jielong Lu,Zhihao Wu,Jiajun Yu,Zhaoliang Chen,Haishuai Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SIMPLE，一个先验拟合的多视图上下文学习器，将多视图学习重新定义为可复用的、任务条件化的推理过程，而非固定的融合函数。通过在嵌入空间中构造可控的合成任务先验，训练层次化推理架构，使冻结的推理骨干无需更新即可在下游任务上取得有竞争力的性能，轻量级adapter校准可达到领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练编码器使得异构视图的表示越来越可复用，但决定视图效用并组合证据的过程仍需为每个下游任务重新学习，导致视图相关性、互补性、可靠性及缺失性知识在任务间被反复丢弃而非迁移。作者希望将多视图推理本身作为可预训练和可复用的能力。

Method: 1) 将多视图学习重构为任务条件化的可复用推理过程（类似上下文学习范式）；2) 提出SIMPLE（prior-fitted multi-view in-context learner），通过小规模标注支持集条件化预测查询标签；3) 构建可控的合成任务先验，生成多样化的支持-查询episode，涵盖不同类别结构、共享/视图专有因子、几何形状、跨视图依赖、可靠性、缺失模式和分布偏移；4) 采用层次化推理架构，在视图内、跨视图、跨支持-查询样本三个层面进行推理；5) 支持冻结推理骨干和轻量级adapter校准两种模式。

Result: 在多视图和多组学基准上，SIMPLE的冻结变体在不更新推理骨干的情况下达到有竞争力的性能；采用轻量级adapter校准后在大多数评测数据集上取得领先性能。在冻结、单样本和缺失视图设置下的结果均支持核心假设。

Conclusion: 多视图推理本身可以像编码器一样被预训练并在任务间复用，轻量级adapter校准可在需要时提供任务特定的对齐，从而验证了"多视图推理可预训练"的核心假设。

Abstract: Modern pretrained encoders make representations from heterogeneous views increasingly reusable, but the procedure that determines view utility and combines evidence is still relearned for each downstream task. Consequently, knowledge about view relevance, complementarity, reliability, and missingness is repeatedly discarded rather than transferred across tasks. We therefore reformulate multi-view learning as learning a reusable, task-conditioned inference procedure rather than a fixed fusion function. Based on this perspective, we propose SIMPLE, a prior-fitted multi-view in-context learner that predicts query labels by conditioning on a small labeled support set. Since existing real-world datasets cover only a limited range of view configurations and task structures, we construct a controllable synthetic task prior in embedding space. It generates diverse support-query episodes with varying class structures, shared and view-specific factors, representation geometries, cross-view dependencies, reliability levels, missingness patterns, and distribution shifts. A hierarchical inference architecture then performs reasoning within views, across views, and across support and query samples. Experiments on multi-view and multi-omics benchmarks demonstrate that the frozen variant of SIMPLE achieves competitive performance without updating the inference backbone, while lightweight adapter calibration attains leading performance on most evaluated datasets. Together, the results under frozen, one-shot, and missing-view settings support the central hypothesis that multi-view reasoning itself can be pretrained and reused, while lightweight adapter calibration provides task-specific alignment when needed.

</details>


### [180] [Enhancing EBSD throughput of battery electrode materials using super-resolution generative adversarial networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19117)
*John Mangum,Andrew Glaws,Francois Usseglio-Viretta,Steven Spurgeon,Donal Finegan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于生成对抗网络（SRGAN）的超分辨率框架，用于显著提升锂离子电池电极材料NMC阴极颗粒的EBSD表征通量。经过5倍上采样（对应25倍采集速度提升）时，晶粒面积等效直径、最大内接球直径和晶界长度的相对误差分别为+5.7%、+8.2%和-14.6%，在保持关键微结构指标精度的同时实现了高通量表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EBSD是锂离子电池电极材料微观结构定量表征的关键方法，但其固有的采集速度慢限制了统计代表性分析的通量，需要一种方法来加速EBSD采集同时在关键微结构参数上保持可接受的精度。

Method: 采用SRGAN（超分辨率生成对抗网络）作为机器学习超分辨率框架，在NMC正极颗粒的EBSD数据上进行训练，将低分辨率数据集计算增强为高分辨率数据，并与经典插值方法在2倍至12倍的上采样因子范围内进行系统对比。

Result: SRGAN在所有上采样因子下均系统性地优于经典插值方法，尤其在保留小晶粒和保持真实晶界方面表现突出。5倍上采样（对应25倍采集加速或25倍视场扩大）被验证为实用选择，此时晶粒面积等效直径、最大内接球直径和晶界长度相对误差分别为+5.7%、+8.2%和-14.6%。

Conclusion: 该SRGAN方法显著提升了EBSD采集效率，使其能够获取更具统计鲁棒性的微结构数据集，将EBSD确立为材料研究和工业过程开发中的高通量表征工具。

Abstract: Quantitative microstructural characterization of Li-ion battery electrode materials using electron backscatter diffraction (EBSD) has been proven as a critical method for optimizing cell performance. However, the inherently slow nature of EBSD can hinder the throughput of analyses needed for statistical representation of a material microstructure being developed. This work demonstrates a machine learning super-resolution framework using a generative adversarial network (SRGAN) to significantly increase EBSD throughput. The SRGAN model was trained on EBSD data of LiNixMnyCozO2 (NMC) cathode particles to computationally enhance low-resolution datasets and its performance is compared against classical interpolation methods across various upscaling factors (2x to 12x). Both qualitative image metrics and quantitative microstructural analysis verified that the SRGAN systematically outperformed classical methods, particularly in preserving small grains and maintaining realistic grain boundaries. We demonstrate that a 5x upscaling factor, corresponding to a 25x speed-up in acquisition time or a 25x larger field of view, is practical while maintaining acceptable accuracy in key metrics like grain size and shape. For instance, at 5x upscaling, relative errors were +5.7%, +8.2%, and -14.6% on grain area-equivalent diameter, grain maximum sphere-inscribed diameter, and grain boundary length, respectively. The SRGAN methodology developed in this work significantly enhances the efficiency of EBSD acquisition for more statistically robust microstructural dataset, enabling EBSD as a high-throughput characterization tool for materials research and industrial process development.

</details>


### [181] [Discretizing Continuous Time Series for Imputation with Masked Diffusion Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19119)
*Dongbin Kim,Seungyun Lee,Geonwoo Shin,Jaewook Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MDTIM，一种基于掩码扩散模型的时间序列插补方法，通过将MASK token与有效观测在表示空间中正交分离，并直接预测原始信号而非噪声，同时引入随机离散化（Stochastic Discretization）将连续值映射为有序感知令牌，从而同时对齐了表示空间和学习目标与插补任务，在多个基准上超越现有SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列插补方法存在两个局限：(1) 缺失值与观测值被嵌入到同一表示空间中，缺乏显式结构分离；(2) 基于连续扩散的方法训练目标是预测添加的噪声而非原始信号，与插补任务本身不一致。

Method: MDTIM采用掩码扩散模型的训练范式：MASK token被设计为与有效观测在结构上正交，模型直接预测原始值。此外，引入随机离散化机制，将连续有序的时间序列值映射为保留顺序信息的离散令牌，同时维持连续动态特性，弥合离散掩码扩散与连续标量时间序列之间的鸿沟。

Result: 在多种基准数据集和不同缺失场景下，MDTIM在鲁棒性和可扩展性上表现出色，持续优于现有最先进的确定性方法和生成式插补基线。

Conclusion: 通过在表示空间（正交MASK token）和目标函数（直接预测原始值）两个层面与插补任务对齐，并辅以随机离散化连接连续与离散域，MDTIM为时间序列插补提供了更自然、更有效的建模方案。

Abstract: Time series imputation is a crucial area for reliable time series analysis, yet it remains challenging due to the complex temporal dynamics and noise of real-world data. Existing approaches, however, exhibit two limitations: missing and observed values are embedded within the same representation space without explicit structural separation, and continuous diffusion-based methods are trained to predict added noise rather than the original signal. To address these, we propose the Masked Diffusion Time-series Imputation Model (MDTIM), which leverages the training paradigm of masked diffusion model for imputation tasks. The MASK token is structurally orthogonal to valid observations, and the model directly predicts the original values, naturally aligning both the representation and the learning objective with the imputation task. To bridge the gap between discrete masked diffusion and the continuous, ordinal nature of time series, we further introduce Stochastic Discretization, which maps continuous values to ordinal-aware tokens while preserving continuous dynamics. Our experiments on diverse benchmarks confirm that MDTIM achieves superior robustness and scalability, consistently outperforming state-of-the-art deterministic and generative baselines across various missing scenarios.

</details>


### [182] [PGFS++: Molecular Property Improvement under Synthesis and Diversity Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19121)
*Boqiao Zhang,Godbless James,Sai Krishna Gottipati,Andrew Fitzgibbon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了PGFS++框架，一种合成感知的强化学习方法，用于分子改进任务。该方法以输入分子为前向合成轨迹的起点，应用学习的反应模板和兼容的库存构建模块，生成具有改进目标性质、明确合成路线且与输入结构相似的新分子。实验表明PGFS++在提升目标性质的同时保持了高输出多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子优化方法多在无约束化学空间中进行，优化得到的分子往往难以合成，实用价值有限。虽然之前的PGFS方法引入了合成感知的强化学习，但其反应物嵌入预测机制使反应物选择过于间接，限制了学习效率。此外，改进后的PGFS+虽然显著提升了目标性质，却暴露了奖励黑客问题——强大的反应物搜索会将多样的输入分子映射到同一个高奖励分子上，导致输出多样性崩溃。因此需要一种既能考虑合成可行性又能保持输入特异性的方法。

Method: PGFS++采用合成感知的强化学习框架：1）将输入分子视为前向合成轨迹的起点；2）使用可训练嵌入查找表表示反应模板和第二反应物（改进自PGFS+）；3）应用与库存构建模块兼容的学习反应模板进行合成扩展；4）通过更有效的评分函数和强化学习算法优化目标性质；5）通过保持与输入分子的结构相似性来约束搜索空间，防止奖励黑客导致的输出多样性collapse。

Result: 在分子改进任务上的实验表明，PGFS++在提升目标性质方面表现优异，同时保持了高输出多样性。与PGFS和PGFS+相比，PGFS++有效解决了奖励黑客问题，确保了输出分子与输入分子的结构相似性，并且生成的分子具有明确的合成路线。

Conclusion: PGFS++通过将输入分子作为前向合成轨迹的起点，结合可训练嵌入表示和结构相似性约束，在分子改进任务中实现了目标性质提升与输出多样性的平衡。该框架为合成感知的分子优化提供了更实用、更可靠的解决方案，避免了奖励黑客导致的多样性崩溃问题。

Abstract: Improving molecular properties, such as drug-likeness or binding affinity, is a recurring task in early-stage drug discovery. However, molecules optimized in an unconstrained chemical space have limited practical value if they cannot be synthesized. Policy Gradient for Forward Synthesis (PGFS) is a synthesis-aware reinforcement learning method for molecular improvement, but its use of reactant embedding prediction makes reactant selection indirect, which, as we show, limits learning effectiveness. We first develop PGFS+, in which reaction templates and second reactants are represented by trainable embedding lookup tables. Combined with a more effective scoring function and RL algorithm, PGFS+ significantly improves the desired property. However, it exposes a reward-hacking failure mode: a powerful reactant search can map diverse input molecules to the same high-reward magnet molecule, improving the reward while collapsing the output diversity. We therefore introduce PGFS++, a synthesis-aware reinforcement learning framework for input-specific molecular improvement. Given an input molecule, PGFS++ treats it as the start of a forward-synthesis trajectory, applies learned reaction templates with compatible in-stock building blocks, and produces a molecule with improved target properties, an explicit synthesis route, and structural similarity to the input. Experiments on molecular improvement tasks show that PGFS++ improves target properties while preserving high output diversity.

</details>


### [183] [Beyond Teacher Likelihood: Group-Calibrated On-Policy Distillation for Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19181)
*Zhu Zhang,Jixun Wang,Xiaoang Xu,Xiaorong Wang,Zihan Zhou,Zhiyuan Wang,Shuo Wang,Chaojun Xiao,Yuezhi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GC-OPD（分组校准在线蒸馏），通过在rollout组内分别归一化verifier奖励和轨迹级OPD分数，利用其有符号差值作为师生分歧残差，并采用相对优势信用分配（RACA）将轨迹级残差分配到各token，在五个长上下文基准上将Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-8B的平均分从29.08/35.12提升至40.47/44.65，优于原始OPD（39.31/43.56）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线蒸馏（OPD）在长上下文任务中存在根本性缺陷：token级教师指导倾向于偏好局部看似合理的回答，而忽略了分布在输入各处或违反全局任务约束的信息。与此相对，任务级verifier在响应层面评估任务完成度，能返回反映部分成功的分级奖励。作者发现了"教师-验证器不一致"问题：在更长输入范围内，轨迹级OPD分数与verifier奖励的对齐程度逐渐降低，说明两者评判标准存在系统性分歧，这需要被显式建模和校准。

Method: GC-OPD的核心创新包括两部分：(1) 在每组rollout内分别对verifier奖励和轨迹级OPD分数进行归一化，计算二者之差作为有符号的教师-验证器分歧残差；(2) 通过相对优势信用分配（RACA），根据各token的相对OPD优势将轨迹级残差分配到token层面，同时保留原始OPD信号不被丢弃。这一设计兼顾了dense token级指导教师与sparse response级verifier反馈两者的优点。

Result: 在五个长上下文基准上，GC-OPD将官方Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-8B检查点的五基准平均分从29.08和35.12分别提升至40.47和44.65；而原始OPD在同一设定下仅达到39.31和43.56。消融实验表明：(1) 有符号残差比额外加入OPD衍生项或直接加组归一化verifier奖励更有效；(2) RACA优于均匀token分配。

Conclusion: 组内相对残差校准能够在不舍弃dense token级指导的前提下有效融入verifier结果，为在线蒸馏与verifier反馈的融合提供了一种可行的解决方案。该方法在长上下文证据聚合任务上系统性优于原始OPD。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own responses using dense token-level guidance from a stronger teacher. In long-context tasks, however, token-level teacher support can favor locally plausible responses that omit evidence distributed across the input or violate global task constraints. Task-specific verifiers, in contrast, evaluate task completion at the response level and may return graded rewards that reflect partial success. We diagnose this mismatch on fixed responses from two representative long-context evidence-aggregation tasks. Across longer input ranges, trajectory-level OPD scores become progressively less aligned with verifier rewards, indicating teacher-verifier disagreement. Motivated by this observation, we introduce Group-Calibrated On-Policy Distillation (GC-OPD). GC-OPD separately normalizes verifier rewards and trajectory-level OPD scores within each rollout group and uses their difference as a signed teacher-verifier disagreement residual. Relative-advantage-based credit assignment (RACA) distributes this trajectory-level residual across tokens according to their relative OPD advantages while preserving the original OPD signal. Across five long-context benchmarks, post-training with GC-OPD raises the five-benchmark averages of the official Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B checkpoints from 29.08 to 40.47 and from 35.12 to 44.65, respectively. Vanilla OPD reaches 39.31 and 43.56 under the same setup. Controlled ablations show that the signed residual is more effective than either an additional OPD-derived term or direct group-normalized verifier reward addition, while RACA further improves over uniform token allocation. Together, these results demonstrate that group-relative residual calibration can incorporate verifier outcomes without discarding dense token-level guidance. Code is available at https://github.com/SolereZhang/GC-OPD.

</details>


### [184] [Leaf Values as Coordinates: Exact Contrastive Explanation for Gradient-Boosted Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19127)
*Emanuele Luzio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将梯度提升集成模型的叶节点输出值重新解读为坐标，使每个样本成为R^M空间中的点，模型在该空间上线性作用。这种视角转换使对比性解释变得精确且可计算，并基于此构建了一个可操作（recourse）方法，在五个表格数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度提升树虽然强大但难以解释，尤其是对比性解释（如为什么一个申请人被拒而另一个被接受）通常依赖启发式方法或近似手段。现有方法要么假设特征可加性，要么需要采样或拟合，无法给出精确、可追溯的解释。

Method: 将梯度提升模型中每棵树输出的叶节点值视为高维空间(R^M)中的坐标而非中间结果。每个实例映射为该空间的一个点，模型得分就是坐标之和，实际上是线性操作。两个实例的差异向量在共享叶子的坐标处为零，因此被拒绝与接受申请者之间的差距仅由少数坐标承载，每个坐标可追溯到具体的树和分裂。

Result: 在五个表格数据集上通过重复交叉验证评估：推荐结果重建模型自身决策的误差达6.2×10^-15（可由审计人员无模型验证）；在信用数据集上，努力与实真性维度上帕累托最优；当推荐限制为只包含受试者实际可执行的变更时，该方法保留了58%的有效性，而最强基线仅保留41%。

Conclusion: 通过将叶节点值重新解读为坐标，梯度提升模型的对比性解释可以从根本上变得精确而非近似。该表示天然支持可操作性评估，标准评估方法无法发现的可执行性问题，在此框架下得以揭示和解决。

Abstract: A gradient-boosted ensemble predicts by summing one leaf value per tree. Read
  those values as coordinates rather than as intermediate results, and every
  instance becomes a point in R^M on which the model acts linearly: the score is
  the sum of the coordinates.
  This small change of view makes contrastive explanation exact. The difference
  between two instances is a vector that is identically zero wherever they share
  a leaf, so the gap between a rejected applicant and an accepted one is carried
  by a handful of coordinates, each traceable to a real split in a real tree.
  Nothing is fitted, sampled, or assumed additive in features -- the additivity
  is already there, in the right space.
  We build a recourse method on this representation and evaluate it on five
  tabular datasets under repeated cross-validation. Its recommendation
  reconstructs the model's own decision to 6.2 x 10^-15, so an auditor can
  re-check the arithmetic without the model. On the credit datasets it is
  Pareto-non-dominated on effort against realism. And when recommendations are
  restricted to changes the subject could actually make -- not their age, not a
  settled delinquency -- it retains 58% of its validity where the strongest
  baseline retains 41%, a distinction the standard evaluation cannot see because
  it never asks whether a recommendation can be carried out.

</details>


### [185] [Beyond Trial Averaging: Anchoring Neural and Visual Representations for Few-Repetition Brain-to-Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19128)
*Zhenyao Cui,Siyuan Kan,Dingkun Liu,Dongrui Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NEAR框架，通过将高重复平均中心作为锚点，从查询端和图像端双向逼近，解决脑电/脑磁/功能磁共振成像检索中低重复次数下准确率骤降的问题，在四个数据集上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脑-图像检索方法通常需要平均多达80次神经试次才能获得良好性能，导致延迟高、成本大、用户负担重。当只有单次或少量重复时检索准确率急剧下降。以往研究将这一下降归因于查询噪声，但本文发现存在非传递对齐模式——低重复查询和高重复中心对齐、图像表示也与高重复中心对齐，但两者彼此并不直接对齐，说明查询噪声只是问题的一部分，图库放置（gallery placement）也影响检索效果。

Method: 提出神经锚点检索（NEAR）框架：将高重复中心视为锚点，从两方面逼近——（1）去噪器将带噪的查询信号拉向真实锚点；（2）一个小型网络从图像预测每个候选的伪锚点，并将图像表示拉向该伪锚点。通过在神经表征和视觉表征两个侧面对齐到共同锚点，实现低重复条件下的有效检索。

Result: 在覆盖EEG、MEG和fMRI的四个数据集上，NEAR在少重复（few-repetition）场景下均一致地提升了检索性能。在THINGS-EEG2上，平均1次和4次重复时，200路Top-1准确率分别提升了5.7和9.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过将神经表征和视觉表征锚定到高重复中心，NEAR减少了对重复采集的依赖，使脑-图像检索更接近实际部署应用。

Abstract: Decoding visual information from brain signals probes neural representations and enables neuro-rehabilitation and dream decoding. Recent brain-to-image retrieval approaches have achieved promising performance, typically by averaging many (up to 80) neural trials per image, requiring repeated stimulus presentation that increases latency, cost, and user burden. When only one or a few repetitions are available, the retrieval accuracy drops sharply. This drop is commonly attributed to query noise because averaging suppresses noise and increases signal stability. However, we find a non-transitive alignment pattern: the low-repetition query signal and the image representation each align with the high-repetition center, but not directly with each other. This pattern shows that query noise is only part of the problem and that gallery placement also affects retrieval. We therefore propose a neural-anchor-based retrieval (NEAR) framework that treats the high-repetition center as an anchor and approaches it from both sides: a denoiser pulls the noisy query toward the true anchor, and a small network predicts each candidate's pseudo anchor from its image and pulls the image toward it. Across four datasets spanning EEG, MEG and fMRI, NEAR consistently improved retrieval in the few-repetition regime. On THINGS-EEG2, it improved 200-way Top-1 accuracy by 5.7 and 9.3 percentage points respectively, when averaging one and four repetitions. By anchoring neural and visual representations, NEAR reduces reliance on repeated acquisition and brings neural retrieval closer to real-world deployment.

</details>


### [186] [SCORE: Subject Coordinate Recovery for Label-Free Cross-Subject EEG-to-Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19134)
*Zhenyao Cui,Siyuan Kan,Siyang Li,Ziwei Wang,Dongrui Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SCORE（Subject Coordinate Recovery）框架，通过恢复-感知源域训练与部署时的坐标对齐，实现无需目标标签的跨被试脑电-图像检索，在THINGS-EEG2和Alljoined-1.6M两个基准上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前EEG到图像检索方法对新用户（无标注校准数据）的性能远低于同被试场景，严重限制了实际部署。作者发现不同被试间概念关系相似但表达方向（坐标方向）不同，这导致直接迁移性能下降，亟需一种无需标签即可进行跨被试适配的方案。

Method: SCORE包含两部分：1）训练阶段：通过恢复感知的源域训练将源被试EEG与共享图像空间对齐，并以源域单独样本模拟未见被试的恢复过程；2）部署阶段：在编码器均冻结的情况下，利用hubness校正的匹配策略选取可靠的EEG-图像地标对，进而估计正交变换以恢复目标被试的EEG坐标，全程无需源数据或目标标签。

Result: 在200路检索任务中，SCORE对每个目标被试均优于未适配基线，取得最佳总体精度。THINGS-EEG2上Top-1/Top-5达53.23%/83.55%，Alljoined-1.6M上达12.01%/32.16%，相较最强基线分别提升17.45/15.70和3.08/4.62个百分点。

Conclusion: SCORE在无需目标标签、不更新编码器的条件下，使EEG视觉解码的跨被试泛化能力显著提升，推动了基于大脑信号的视觉解码向稳健、实用、低延迟的实际部署迈进。

Abstract: Accurate visual decoding can reveal how the brain represents visual information and recover perceived content from neural signals such as electroencephalography (EEG), with potential for neural communication. However, current EEG-to-image retrieval methods perform far below their within-subject counterparts for new users without labeled calibration, limiting real-world deployment. To understand this gap, we analyze EEG features across subjects and find that different subjects preserve similar relationships among concepts but express them along different coordinate directions. We therefore propose Subject Coordinate Recovery (SCORE), a target label-free framework combining recovery-aware source training with coordinate alignment at deployment. During training, SCORE aligns source subject EEG with a common image space and simulates unseen-subject recovery through source-only episodes. At deployment, with both encoders frozen, SCORE selects reliable EEG-image landmarks through hubness-corrected matching and estimates an orthogonal transformation to recover target EEG coordinates without source data or target labels. In 200-way retrieval on two public benchmarks, SCORE outperforms the unadapted baseline for every target subject and achieves the best overall accuracy. It reaches 53.23%/83.55% and 12.01%/32.16% Top-1/Top-5 on THINGS-EEG2 and Alljoined-1.6M, respectively, surpassing the strongest baselines by 17.45/15.70 and 3.08/4.62 percentage points. Without target labels or encoder updates, SCORE brings brain-based visual decoding closer to robust, practical, low-latency deployment across users.

</details>


### [187] [Bridge Graphical Models: Coupling, Projection, and Current-Preserving Dynamics for Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19144)
*Tiantian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文识别出连续时间生成模型中的"桥到解码器压缩"这一结构性瓶颈，提出"马尔可夫化差距"（Markovization gap）作为预训练诊断指标，并引入桥图模型（BGMs）统一形式化框架来比较不同模型族的这一瓶颈，实验证明该指标可在训练前预测下游性能排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间生成模型通常由端点条件的桥构造，但实际生成需要使用仅观测当前状态和时间的非前瞻性马尔可夫解码器。这一桥到解码器的压缩过程是扩散模型、流匹配、整流流、薛定谔桥和基于场的生成模型共同面临的结构性瓶颈，但此前缺乏统一的度量方式来量化该瓶颈的大小及其对各模型族的影响。

Method: 作者引入马尔可夫化差距（Markovization gap），定义为给定马尔可夫状态条件下的桥速度的时间积分条件方差，即从采样器可获得信息预测端点条件运动的MMSE。为跨模型族比较该瓶颈，提出桥图模型（BGMs）框架，将端点耦合、桥定律、马尔可夫投影和保持电流的动力学表示作为独立设计选择分离。该形式同样将泊松模型和静电模型表示为场线桥核，并给出对应的场线马尔可夫化差距。通过特征空间代理差距在训练前数分钟内估算设计选择的方向。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和Fashion-MNIST上的合成、潜在和像素空间实验中，特征空间代理差距在训练前估计的设计选择排序与固定架构、桥、采样器和计算条件下的下游训练损失和FID方向一致。

Conclusion: 马尔可夫化差距可作为桥与耦合设计的预训练诊断指标，它量化了在任何神经网络训练之前就已发生的不可约损失，为比较不同连续时间生成模型族的桥设计选择提供了理论基础和实用工具。

Abstract: Continuous-time generative models are often built from endpoint-conditioned bridges, but generation requires a different object: a non-anticipative Markov decoder that only observes the current state and time. We identify this bridge-to-decoder compression as a structural bottleneck shared by diffusion models, flow matching, rectified flow, Schrödinger bridges, and field-based generative models. We introduce the \emph{Markovization gap}, the time-integrated conditional variance of the bridge velocity given the Markov state. It is the MMSE of predicting endpoint-conditioned motion from the information available to a sampler, and it measures an irreducible loss incurred before any neural network is trained. To make this bottleneck comparable across model families, we define \emph{Bridge Graphical Models} (BGMs), which separate endpoint coupling, bridge law, Markovian projection, and current-preserving dynamics representation as independent design choices. The same formalism also represents Poisson and electrostatic models as field-line bridge kernels with a corresponding field-line Markovization gap. Across synthetic, latent, and pixel-space pilots on CIFAR-10 and Fashion-MNIST, a feature-space proxy gap estimated in minutes before training ranks design choices in the same direction as downstream training loss and FID under fixed architecture, bridge, sampler, and compute. These results support the Markovization gap as a pre-training diagnostic for bridge and coupling design.

</details>


### [188] [Learned, Then Lost: A Measured Single-Example Counterfactual in Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19168)
*Zachary Speck,Asa Shepard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过在124M参数的GPT-2模型上执行24次全预训练反事实实验，测量单个训练样本对最终模型的真实影响。结果显示：样本在一次曝光后被学习但随后衰减，最终未见可检测的持久影响；模型权重位移可达种子间距离的44.1%，但损失势垒仅达3.0%，说明注入在盆地内重新定位了模型而未将其移出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单个训练样本对最终模型的贡献通常只能估计而非精确测量，因为精确测量需要两次仅在一个batch的一行上不同的昂贵完整预训练。作者希望通过实际执行这种反事实实验来获得直接的因果证据。

Method: 从零开始训练32个124M参数的GPT-2模型（四条件×八种子）于OpenWebText上。在训练第200步（峰值学习率）时，将256行batch中的一行替换为携带194-token文章的固定上下文注入。四个条件为：流畅散文（真实主题）、流畅散文（伪造主题，与真实主题在全batch梯度差异0.14%内匹配）、随机键盘字符、以及无注入对照组。测量包括交叉熵、插值损失势垒、留出集交叉熵、逐层中心核对齐（CKA）及权重位移等几何指标。

Result: 注入后50步，注入条件的模型对文章的预测比对照好0.039和0.044 nats（8/8种子，p<10⁻⁴），但在最终步骤未检测到差异（p=0.25/0.71，最小可检测效应0.025/0.079 nats），两文章间差异p=0.54。预注册的插值势垒对比+0.0068（p=0.509），留出交叉熵-0.00044（p=0.310），CKA在任何层均无显著差异。权重位移达种子间欧几里得距离的44.1%，且训练中点时已安定92%；势垒达种子间势垒的3.0%，两者约相差15倍。

Conclusion: 单个训练样本在模型上的影响是暂时性的：学习后迅速衰减，不留下可检测的持久几何痕迹。注入使模型在其损失盆地内部重新定位，但不会将其移动到盆地之外；权重位移与势垒之间的15倍差距表明单个样本的影响被限制在局部，无法在最终模型中被持久保留或检测。

Abstract: A single training example's contribution to a finished model is normally estimated rather than measured, because measuring it takes two expensive full pre-training runs that differ in one row of one batch. We ran that counterfactual 24 times at a small scale. We trained 32 GPT-2 models at 124M parameters from scratch on OpenWebText, over four conditions and eight seeds. At step 200 of 9,536, at peak learning rate, we replaced one row of a 256-row batch with a fixed context injection carrying a 194-token passage. The three injected conditions are: 1. fluent prose with a corpus-attested subject, 2. fluent prose with a fabricated subject matched to it within 0.14% on full-batch gradient delta, and 3. random keyboard characters. The fourth condition is an uninjected twin. The passage is learned from one exposure and then decays. Fifty steps after injection, the arm that saw a passage predicts it better than the arm that did not by 0.039 and 0.044 nats of cross-entropy on the passage, at eight of eight seeds with p < $10^{-4}$. At the final step we do not detect that difference for either passage, at p = 0.25 and p = 0.71, against minimum detectable effects of 0.025 and 0.079 nats, nor between the two passages, at p=0.54. Every geometric measure we report is taken after that decay. Our pre-registered contrast on interpolation loss barrier is +0.0068 with p = 0.509, against a minimum detectable effect of 0.032 barrier units. Held-out cross-entropy is $-0.00044$ with p = 0.310. Per-layer centered kernel alignment does not detectably separate any condition at any layer. Weight displacement reaches 44.1% of the seed-to-seed Euclidean distance and is 92% settled by the midpoint of training, while the barrier reaches 3.0% of the seed-to-seed barrier. Those two figures sit roughly 15 times apart, and that is a lower bound. The injection relocates the model within its basin without moving it out.

</details>


### [189] [Lévy Attention: Single-Pass Predictive Uncertainty for Continuous-Time Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19171)
*Sotirios P. Chatzis,Loukas Papadoulas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Lévy Attention，一种基于非齐次泊松随机测度的随机积分交叉注意力算子，在不增加任何额外成本的情况下，通过确定性前向传播即可输出每个预测的不确定性度量（证据度和分歧度组合成的均方根偏差）。该算子期望上退化为mollified余弦核注意力，可替代softmax层并以精确梯度训练，在不规则采样时间序列上实现了比20-pass MC dropout更好的不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度模型可以回答任意连续时间戳上的查询，但无法报告每个预测结果应被信任的程度。现有不确定性估计方法（如MC dropout）需要多次采样，成本高昂。作者观察到注意力层本身可以弥合这一差距——通过合适的随机公式，产生预测的前向传播同时可以闭式报告不确定性，无需额外的训练头。

Method: 引入Lévy Attention：一种跨注意力算子，其输出是相对于非齐次泊松随机测度的随机积分。查询-键兼容性在连续的时间×通道索引空间上组装强度函数，测度在其下散布原子，输出对这些原子处的插值值场求平均。其期望退化为mollified余弦核注意力（可替代softmax层），且可精确梯度训练。泊松构造以闭式保留了softmax丢弃的证据量Λ_q（总兼容性质量）和分歧度tr Σ_V(q)（值扩展），通过精确方差恒等式得到\hatσ(q)=√(tr Σ_V(q)·φ(Λ_q))作为采样算子的均方根偏差，由确定性前向传播免费输出。

Result: 经验表明分歧度携带主要信号，而证据因子在密集数据上无信息、在稀疏数据上高度信息。在t-PatchGNN上，算子替换相对于匹配对照组的准确率损失最多5.6%，在最稀疏数据集上无损失。免费分歧度信号在匹配的五种子套件上优于20-pass MC dropout；\hatσ校准的高斯分布在零采样CRPS上击败五十次采样器；分裂共形包装器在每个水平达到标称覆盖率；单次前向在1.4秒内对3,383个未见患者进行信任排名。

Conclusion: 通过巧妙的泊松随机测度公式化，注意力层不仅生成预测，还在不增加计算成本的情况下充当自带的不确定性估计器。分歧度（而非证据量）是主要的不确定性信号，且在稀疏数据上证据因子同样有意义。该方法在预测性能上几乎无损（尤其稀疏场景零损失），同时在不确定性质量上显著超越MC dropout，并支持校准、共形覆盖和高效的大规模患者排序。

Abstract: Deep models for irregularly-sampled time series answer queries at arbitrary continuous timestamps, yet report nothing about how far each answer should be trusted. We show the attention layer itself can close that gap: with the right stochastic formulation, the pass that makes each prediction also reports, in closed form and at no extra cost, how far it should be trusted. We introduce Lévy Attention, a cross-attention operator whose output is a stochastic integral against an inhomogeneous Poisson random measure: query-key compatibilities assemble an intensity over a continuous (time x channel) index space, the measure scatters atoms under it, and the output averages an interpolated value field at those atoms. In expectation it reduces to a mollified cosine-kernel attention, so it replaces a softmax layer and trains with exact gradients.
  What softmax discards, the Poisson construction preserves in closed form: the evidence $Λ_q$ (total compatibility mass) and the disagreement $\mathrm{tr}\,Σ_V(q)$ (value spread). An exact variance identity makes their combination $\hatσ(q)=\sqrt{\mathrm{tr}\,Σ_V(q)\,\varphi(Λ_q)}$ the root-mean-square deviation of the sampled operator, emitted by the deterministic pass with no trained head.
  Empirically, disagreement carries the signal, while the evidence factor swings from uninformative on dense data to strongly informative on sparse. On t-PatchGNN the operator swap costs at most 5.6% accuracy against a matched control and nothing on the sparsest dataset. The free disagreement signal improves on 20-pass MC dropout across matched five-seed suites, and $\hatσ$ scales a calibrated Gaussian whose zero-sample CRPS beats a fifty-draw sampler; a split-conformal wrapper reaches nominal coverage at every level, and one pass ranks 3,383 unseen patients by trust in 1.4 seconds.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [190] [Position: Collusion Risks Among AI Reasoning Agents Justify Certification Requirements for Making Market Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18078)
*Matthew Riemer,Tommaso Tosato,Amin Memarian,Maximilian Puelma Touzel,Glen Berseth,Irina Rish,Guillaume Dumas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证具有链式推理（Chain-of-Thought）能力的AI智能体天然倾向于合谋行为，提出在将其部署于经济市场决策前必须进行行为认证。实验表明DeepSeek-R1在Bertrand寡头定价场景中表现出即使有人类提示禁止合谋仍持续存在的默示合谋倾向，且链式推理可被引导至极端合谋或激烈竞争而无法被其他LLM语义检测，因此需要基于代表性情境的观察行为认证来防范合谋。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着具备推理能力的AI智能体逐步融入经济市场，其可能破坏法律中区分竞争与合谋的证据性基础（即无法获取合谋的"证据"或"意图"），但同时不会削弱经济危害的区分。这种法律失效与经济损失并存的风险，促使作者论证必须对推理型AI智能体进行行为认证。

Method: 采用立场论文（position paper）形式，结合实验验证。实验使用DeepSeek-R1智能体在Bertrand寡头定价领域进行博弈实验，检验其默示合谋倾向、人类提示对合谋的抑制效果、链式推理被引导至不同竞争程度的行为，以及用另一个LLM分析推理痕迹以检验语义可检测性。此外验证了将这些智能体引导至高效竞争均衡的初步可行性。

Result: DeepSeek-R1智能体表现出显著的默示合谋倾向，且人类提示不合作也无法消除该倾向。链式推理可被引导至极端合谋或高度竞争行为，这种引导无法被另一LLM从语义层面检测。部署推理智能体进行市场决策将导致合谋性经济结果，但缺乏任何共谋证据或意图。初步证据表明这些智能体可以被一般化地引导至高效竞争均衡。

Conclusion: 基于观察行为（而非推理内容）的认证机制是防止AI智能体合谋的必要条件。在开发出全面的行为认证体系之前，推理型AI智能体不应被部署于真实市场，以确保市场的稳定性与效率。

Abstract: This position paper argues that AI agents with chain-of-thought reasoning capabilities are predisposed to exhibit collusive behavior and should be required to obtain behavioral certification before making decisions that affect economic markets. This is because integrating these agents into society could collapse the legal evidentiary distinction between competition and collusion among independent firms without eroding the economic harm distinction. Experiments with DeepSeek-R1 agents in the Bertrand oligopoly pricing domain reveal a tendency towards tacit collusion that persists even when humans prompt the agents not to collude. We further show that the chain-of-thought of these agents can be steered toward either extremely collusive or highly competitive behavior in a way that is not semantically detectable by another LLM analyzing the reasoning traces. As a result, deploying reasoning agents for market decisions leads to collusive economic outcomes without any evidence of conspiracy or intent. Thus, certification based on observed behavior in representative situations is necessary to prevent collusion. We provide preliminary evidence that such agents can be steered in a generalizable way toward efficient competitive equilibria. However, developing a comprehensive behavioral certification will be required before these models can be deployed in real-world markets while ensuring their stability and efficiency.

</details>


### [191] [Position: Profiling Game Worlds by Transition Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18079)
*Lele Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Transition Complexity Profile（TCP），一套可复现的指标集，用于量化游戏世界建模与强化学习任务中状态转移预测问题的内在难度，并呼吁其成为标准基准元数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游戏世界建模（GWM）与强化学习（RL）研究中，由于论文很少量化在声明接口（像素/令牌/潜变量，有限历史）下转移预测问题的实际难度，导致两者常被混淆。缺乏标准化难度度量，不同基准之间难以比较。

Method: 构建TCP指标集，包含三个维度：(i) 内在单步分支因子；(ii) 交互诱导的不确定性及可观测时的对手影响；(iii) 通过标准化探测曲线衡量时间/空间依赖跨度。TCP附带明确的参考分布、协议随机性和版本化的测量预算（采样/重采样与固定探针计算量），确保跨基准可比性。

Result: 论文勾勒了常见游戏家族与现代"神经游戏引擎"领域在该难度谱系中的分布情况，展示了TCP作为转移核难度表征工具的有效性，并呼吁将其作为GWM和RL论文中必需的标准统计量。

Conclusion: TCP提供了一套小规模、可复现的指标集来刻画环境转移核的内在难度，应当成为游戏世界建模与强化学习研究的标准基准元数据，以促进跨论文公平比较与领域发展。

Abstract: Game world modeling (GWM) and reinforcement learning (RL) are often confounded because research papers rarely quantify how difficult the underlying transition prediction problem is at the declared interface (pixels/tokens/latents with finite history). We propose the Transition Complexity Profile (TCP): a small, reproducible set of metrics that characterizes an environment's (or gameplay dataset's) induced transition kernel by (i) intrinsic one-step branching, (ii) interaction-induced uncertainty and opponent influence when observable, and (iii) temporal/spatial dependency span via standardized probe curves. TCP is reported with an explicit reference distribution, protocol stochasticity, and a versioned measurement budget (sampling/resampling and fixed probe compute), enabling comparable numbers across benchmarks. We outline how common game families and modern "neural game engine" domains populate this landscape and call for TCP to become standard benchmark metadata and a required statistic in GWM and RL papers.

</details>


### [192] [Large Language Models in Mental Health: A Systematic Review of Applications, Innovations, and Ethical Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18080)
*Yisong Chen,Yifan Gao,Sijing Yu,Chuqing Zhao,Yang Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了大语言模型（LLMs）在心理健康领域的应用，涵盖社交媒体分析、临床对话系统、治疗支持工具、提示工程、多模态学习及伦理考量等方面，整合了多学科研究发现，并讨论了从早期抑郁检测到个性化治疗支持的广泛应用场景及伦理监管挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理健康问题日益严峻，传统诊断和干预方式存在资源不足、可及性差等局限。大语言模型的快速发展为心理健康领域的早期检测、风险评估和个性化干预提供了新机遇，但目前相关研究分散于多学科，缺乏系统性梳理，因此需要一篇综合性综述来整合现状、识别挑战并指引未来方向。

Method: 采用叙述性综述方法，跨学科整合来自社交媒体帖子、电子病历和多模态输入等多样化数据源的研究发现，系统组织并评述LLM在心理健康各应用方向（社交媒体分析、临床对话代理、治疗支持工具、提示工程、多模态学习）的研究进展，同时分析伦理、社会技术和监管层面的挑战。

Result: 综述揭示了LLM在抑郁早期检测、自杀风险评估、个性化治疗支持和心理教育内容生成等方面的显著进展，总结了提升可解释性和临床相关性的模型与标注策略，强调了提示工程对领域适配的关键作用，并指出了文本、语音和传感器数据融合的多模态方法在心理诊断与监测中的潜力。

Conclusion: LLM在心理健康应用中展现出巨大潜力，但需通过框架构建确保其安全、公平和负责任的部署。文章倡导建立兼顾伦理、社会技术和监管要求的系统框架，以推动LLM在真实心理健康护理场景中的可靠落地。

Abstract: We present a review on the applications of large language models (LLMs) in health, e.g., social media analysis, clinical conversational agents, therapy support tools, prompt engineering, multimodal learning, and ethical considerations. We integrate findings from interdisciplinary studies utilizing diverse data sources such as social media posts, electronic medical records, and multimodal inputs to enable early detection of depression, suicide risk assessment, personalized therapy support, and psychoeducational content generation. Our review highlights advancements in LLM models and annotation strategies that enhance interpretability and clinical relevance, while we also emphasize the critical role of prompt engineering for domain adaptation. We also discuss emerging multimodal fusion techniques integrating text, speech, and sensor data for improved mental health diagnosis and monitoring. Finally, we address ongoing ethical, sociotechnical, and regulatory challenges, and advocate frameworks to ensure safe, equitable, and accountable deployment of LLMs in real-world mental health care.

</details>


### [193] [Position: Behavioral Systems Require Behavioral Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18081)
*Manuel Cherep,Nikhil Singh,Pattie Maes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张AI代理应像其他行为系统一样被评估，即通过系统观察、扰动和解释其行为过程，而非仅关注性能结果，并提出建立"AI行为科学"的研究路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI代理评估方法过于聚焦于性能结果（outcomes），忽视了产生这些结果的底层行为过程（behavioral processes）。随着AI代理越来越像行为系统——与动态环境交互、追求目标、随时间适应——亟需更严格的评估范式。

Method: 借鉴行为科学的经验，提出发展严格行为测试的研究议程，具体包括三个方向：(1)从动作序列中恢复决策策略；(2)构建能够隔离行为差异的环境；(3)探测多代理系统中的涌现动态。

Result: 提出了一条发展"AI行为科学"的明确路线图，涵盖方法论层面（行为测试设计）和理论层面（行为解释框架）。

Conclusion: AI代理评估应从纯性能导向转向行为过程导向，通过跨学科借鉴行为科学研究方法，建立系统的AI行为评估框架。

Abstract: Artificial agentic systems increasingly operate as behavioral systems by interacting with dynamic environments, pursuing goals, and adapting over time. Yet, current evaluation methods largely focus on performance outcomes, not the underlying behavioral processes that produce them. This paper argues that AI agents must be evaluated like other behavioral systems: through systematic observation, perturbation, and interpretation of their actions. We draw on lessons from the behavioral sciences to motivate this position, and propose a research agenda focused on developing rigorous behavioral tests. These include methods for recovering decision strategies from action sequences, constructing environments that isolate behavioral differences, and probing emergent dynamics in multi-agent systems. Taken together, these directions offer a roadmap for developing a science of AI behavior.

</details>


### [194] [Position: Current Model Cards Are Insufficient for Downstream Governance of Open-Weight Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18086)
*Sungwon Chae,Keonwoo Kim,Hoki Kim,Jaeyeon Ju,Sangchul Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析Hugging Face上500个模型卡片，提出开源权重基础模型（OWFMs）的有效治理需要多层次方法，整合模型卡片、可接受使用政策（AUPs）和许可证三个互补组件，以弥合现有监管方式在安全方面的缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模型卡片框架虽然被广泛采用为模型仓库中的透明度工具，但未能充分告知下游开发者和用户关于开放权重基础模型（OWFMs）所特有的安全挑战，导致模型治理存在安全缺口。

Method: 这是一篇立场论文（position paper），通过对Hugging Face上托管的500个模型卡片进行实证分析，识别现有监管方法遗留的安全缺口（包括模型谱系、对齐来源和经验观察行为），并论证标准开源许可证（OSLs）不适合OWFMs且可能削弱AUPs的可执行性。

Result: 研究发现现有的模型卡片未能充分提供安全关键信息，标准开源许可证与开放式权重模型不匹配，并可能削弱AUPs的效力。论文提出将模型卡片、AUPs和许可证演变为整合性的安全工件，以形成协调信息、规范和法理维度的综合治理框架。

Conclusion: 开放权重基础模型的有效治理需要多层次方法，将模型卡片、可接受使用政策和许可证整合为连贯的安全工件，构建一个融合信息性、规范性和法律性维度的更全面的治理框架。

Abstract: The growth of open-weight foundation models (OWFMs) has prompted the AI community to re-evaluate strategies for effective downstream governance. Although model cards have been widely adopted as transparency artifacts in model repositories, existing frameworks often fail to adequately inform downstream developers and users about the distinct safety challenges posed by OWFMs. This position paper analyzes 500 model cards hosted on Hugging Face and argues that effective governance of OWFMs requires a multi-layered approach integrating three complementary components: (i) model cards, (ii) acceptable use policies (AUPs), and (iii) licenses. To motivate this claim, we identify a safety gap left by existing regulatory approaches, including model heritage, alignment provenance, and empirically observed behaviors, through an analysis of model cards with safety-critical information. We further argue that standard open-source licenses (OSLs) are not well suited for OWFMs and may weaken the enforceability of AUPs. Building on these observations, we outline directions for evolving model cards, AUPs, and licenses into integrated safety artifacts to enable a more comprehensive governance framework that coherently integrates informational, normative, and legal dimensions.

</details>


### [195] [A Metamorphic Artificial Age Score Decision-Support Prototype for Flight-Log-Based Drone Propeller Health Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18088)
*Seyma Yaman Kayadibi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于飞行日志的无人机螺旋桨健康监测决策支持原型——Metamorphic Artificial Age Score (AAS)，通过计算六个健康相关指标并采用变形充分性关系与冗余调整的AAS公式，对2024 DronePropA公共数据集中的历史飞行日志进行回顾性评估。健康案例被分配常规监测，三个缺陷案例分别被分配维护审查或强制检查，验证了多指标决策支持层对飞行后维护优先级排序和自主系统监督的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机螺旋桨故障的影响可能分布在多个飞行日志通道而非单一诊断信号中，这会产生安全与可靠性风险。传统单一信号诊断方法不足以捕捉此类分布式的故障效应，因此需要一种多指标决策支持方法来有效检测和评估螺旋桨健康状态。

Method: 该框架从原始MATLAB矩阵中计算六个健康相关指标：轨迹跟踪误差、姿态不稳定性、推力指令负担、电机指令不平衡、ESC指令不稳定性和电池水平压力。这些指标相对于健康基线进行归一化，并通过候选评分策略、变形充分性关系（metamorphic adequacy relations）和冗余调整的AAS公式进行评估。AAS在此处被用作结构性策略充分性和负担度量，而非时间年龄度量。实验使用一个健康基线和三个缺陷螺旋桨案例，在相同速度剖面和轨迹下进行受控回顾性评估。

Result: 健康案例被分配常规监测；Severity 1案例以ESC指令不稳定性为主导，被分配维护审查；Severity 2案例达到电机指令和ESC指令最大负担；Severity 3案例达到最大轨迹跟踪误差；Severity 2和3均触发强制检查。结果表明螺旋桨故障效应可能通过不同的运行通道显现。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持在多指标决策支持层进行飞行后维护优先级排序和自主系统监督的必要性，因为螺旋桨故障在飞行日志中表现为多通道分布效应，需要综合多个健康指标进行可靠的健康状态评估。

Abstract: Drone propeller faults can create safety and reliability risks when their effects are distributed across multiple flight-log channels rather than appearing as a single diagnostic signal. This paper proposes a Metamorphic Artificial Age Score (AAS) decision-support prototype for flight-log-based drone propeller health monitoring. Using selected historical real flight logs from the 2024 DronePropA public dataset, the framework computes six health-related indicators from raw MATLAB matrices: trajectory tracking error, attitude instability, thrust-command burden, motor-command imbalance, ESC-command instability, and battery-level stress. These indicators are normalized relative to a healthy baseline and evaluated through candidate scoring policies, metamorphic adequacy relations, and a redundancy-adjusted AAS formulation. In this context, AAS is used as a structural policy-adequacy and burden measure rather than as a chronological age measure. A controlled retrospective evaluation was performed using one healthy baseline and three defective propeller cases under the same speed profile and trajectory. The healthy case was assigned to routine monitoring. The Severity 1 case was dominated by ESC-command instability and assigned to maintenance review. The Severity 2 case reached maximum motor-command and ESC-command burden, while the Severity 3 case reached maximum trajectory tracking error; both triggered mandatory inspection. The results show that propeller fault effects may appear through different operational channels, supporting the need for a multi-indicator decision-support layer for post-flight maintenance prioritization and autonomous-system oversight.

</details>


### [196] [Position: Multi-Agent Systems Should Prioritize Concurrency Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18092)
*Xin Yang,Letian Li,Zimo Ji,Terry Jingchen Zhang,Wenyuan Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个立场观点：基于LLM的多智能体系统（MAS）中的许多失败本质上是并发控制问题，而非单纯的协调或通信问题，主张应将并发控制作为MAS框架的一等设计关注点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统虽然承诺可扩展协作，但增加智能体数量往往降低系统可靠性。许多失败被归因于协调或通信问题，但作者认为这些失败的根本原因在于并发控制缺失。

Method: 通过将MAS中常见的失败模式（如陈旧读取、丢失更新、结果不一致）映射到经典并发控制异常，论证并发控制机制（冲突检测、隔离保证、共享资源的结构化访问）应成为MAS设计的核心组成部分。

Result: 提出MAS失败可归因于并发控制问题，并建议在框架设计中显式引入并发控制机制。

Conclusion: 并发控制应该成为多智能体系统设计中的一等关注点，而非事后补救措施。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) promise scalable collaboration, yet adding agents often reduces reliability. This position paper argues that many MAS failures are fundamentally concurrency control problems: agents concurrently read and write shared state, and long LLM inference windows amplify the risk of stale reads, lost updates, and inconsistent outcomes. Failure modes commonly attributed to coordination or communication breakdowns can be mapped directly onto classical concurrency anomalies. We contend that MAS frameworks should address these failures through explicit concurrency control mechanisms: conflict detection, isolation guarantees, and structured access to shared resources. Concurrency control should be a first-class design concern, not an afterthought.

</details>


### [197] [FinSkillBench: Evaluating AI Agents and Domain Skills for Investment Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18099)
*Jermyn Zhen Yong Bek,Zhuang Qiang Bok,Zhongtian Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FinSkillBench，一个评估语言模型代理在投资管理领域技能调用能力的基准测试套件，涵盖组合构建、风险管理和基本面分析三大领域、12个子任务、2603个任务片段。实验表明，预先策划的技能包能显著提升性能（平均分从0.366提高到0.528），而代理自我生成的技能则收益甚微且计算成本更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投资管理是高风险的决策领域，AI代理系统不仅需要生成合理文本，还必须检索时点数据、组装计算输入、调用专业方法并产出可审计的结构化输出。现有评估体系缺少对代理技能运用能力的系统度量，因此需要专门的基准来评估语言模型代理在金融领域技能使用上的有效性。

Method: 构建FinSkillBench基准，覆盖三大领域12个子任务共2603个任务片段，每个片段包含时点输入、隐藏真值和任务特定验证器。对比三种条件：无技能、预先策划的技能包（含程序文档和可执行组件）、自我生成技能。在9个模型上进行大规模评估，并用独立代理框架Hermes Agent（8个模型、5280个片段）进行复现验证。

Result: 预先策划的技能包在9个模型中稳定提升性能，平均分从0.366提升至0.528，在组合构建和风险管理领域提升最大；自我生成技能虽计算成本更高但收益甚微。独立框架Hermes Agent的5280个片段评估在所有三个领域复现了相同的方向性模式，但技能效果的幅度因子任务和框架而异。

Conclusion: 在投资管理代理中，获取可靠的程序性技能与模型选择同等重要；天真的自我技能生成方法通常无效。研究团队公开了基准、评估工具、策划技能包和完整轨迹以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Investment management is a high-stakes domain in which agentic AI systems must do more than generate plausible text. They must retrieve point-in-time data, assemble correct computational inputs, invoke specialized methods, and produce auditable structured outputs. We introduce FinSkillBench, an evaluation suite designed to measure whether language model agents can effectively use financial domain skills to solve investment management tasks. The benchmark spans three domains, portfolio construction, risk management, and fundamental analysis, and includes 12 subtasks with 2,603 task episodes.
  Each episode provides point-in-time inputs, hidden ground truth, and a task-specific verifier.We compare three conditions: no skill, curated skill packages consisting of procedural documents and executable components, and self-generated skills in which the agent writes and reuses its own procedures within an episode. Across 9 models and a large-scale evaluation, curated skills consistently improve performance, raising mean scores from 0.366 to 0.528, with the largest gains in portfolio construction and risk management.
  In contrast, self-generated skills provide little benefit despite higher computational cost. An independent evaluation using a separate agent framework (Hermes Agent, 8 models, 5,280 episodes total) reproduces the directional pattern across all three domains, with the magnitude of skill effects varying by subtask and harness.
  These results showthat in investment management agents, access to reliable procedural skills can be as important as model choice, while naive self-generation of skills is often ineffective. We release the benchmark, evaluation tools, curated skill packages, and full trajectories to support further research.

</details>


### [198] [Self-Evolving Agents as Dynamic Graph Transformation: A Survey and New Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18104)
*Yuanyuan Xu,Wenjie Zhang,Yin Chen,Xuemin Lin,Ying Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本综述提出将自主进化智能体的状态建模为动态图，将"智能体进化"重新理解为"动态图变换"，并据此构建了包含四类分类法（节点/特征进化、边/拓扑进化、子图激活、跨组件协同进化）的分析框架，同时探讨了九种动态图学习子领域作为智能体进化的可复用基础设施，以及五类图感知的评估与治理协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图-智能体综述将图视为支持智能体功能的结构而非可演化的底物，而自我进化智能体的综述则聚焦于智能体级机制、很少讨论图拓扑的演化。智能体状态（记忆、工具、技能、工作流、智能体间关系）随证据、反馈和环境条件动态变化，但"演化中的智能体状态"与"动态图拓扑"之间的耦合关系尚未被充分探索。本文旨在弥合这两条研究线的鸿沟。

Method: 本文将智能体状态建模为动态图，其中记忆、工具、技能、工作流和智能体间关系被表示为带类型的节点、边和子图，并通过模式约束的重写来更新。基于此形式化框架，将现有基于动态图的自进化智能体方法组织为四类分类法：节点/特征进化、边/拓扑进化、子图激活、跨组件协同进化。进一步提出将动态图学习作为自进化智能体的可复用基础设施，将九个动态图学习子领域映射到智能体演化能力，并讨论其适配方式与可能的失败模式。最后从动态图视角讨论五类图感知的评估与治理协议。

Result: 提出了一套完整的"智能体进化即动态图变换"的概念框架，包括四分类法、九大动态图学习子领域的能力映射、以及五类图感知的评估与治理协议，为设计和治理自进化智能体提供了统一的结构化视角。

Conclusion: 本文旨在通过将智能体演化形式化为动态图变换，提供一个紧凑的结构化透镜，用于设计自进化智能体并对其进行治理，弥补了图-智能体综述与自进化智能体综述之间的研究空白。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents are increasingly becoming self-evolving systems that persist across interactions, maintain memories, use tools, acquire skills, refine workflows, and coordinate with other agents. These capabilities make agent states structural and dynamic: entities, relations, attributes, dependencies, and execution structures change with new evidence, feedback, and environmental conditions. Existing graph-agent surveys typically treat graphs as support structures for agent functions rather than as evolving substrates, while self-evolving-agent surveys focus on agent-level mechanisms and rarely discuss graph topology evolution. Thus, the coupling between evolving agent state and dynamic graph topology remains underexplored. This survey connects these two research lines by framing \textit{agent evolution as dynamic graph transformation}. We model agent state as a dynamic graph, where memories, tools, skills, workflows, and inter-agent relations are represented as typed nodes, edges, and subgraphs updated through schema-constrained rewrites. Based on this formulation, we organize existing dynamic-graph-based methods for self-evolving agents into four taxonomies: node/feature evolution, edge/topology evolution, subgraph activation, and cross-component co-evolution. Building on this taxonomy, we propose dynamic graph learning as reusable infrastructure for self-evolving agents and map nine dynamic-graph-learning subfields to agent-evolution capabilities, discussing their adaptations and possible failure modes. Finally, we discuss five types of graph-aware evaluation and governance protocols from a dynamic-graph perspective, which complement end-task evaluation. The goal is to provide a compact structural lens for designing and governing self-evolving agents.

</details>


### [199] [Emergence of Agentic AI: A Review on Evolution, Background, Working Principles, Applications, Adoption Factors, and Future Research Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18110)
*AKM Bahalul Haque,Al Amin Islam Ridoy,Mohammad Rayhan,Ivan Porres*
